📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期季节性流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 启用 SAM 的 SEM 揭示了 H3N2 病毒颗粒表面明显的“波状”形貌图案,这被解释为临界点干燥收缩导致的制剂诱导伪影。超高压电子断层扫描证实,含有尖峰的包膜结构有助于观察到这种形态。SAM 与负染色的兼容性允许通过不同的包膜变形直接观察乙醇诱导的 H3N2 病毒灭活。
  • 结果体内评估表明,猪对人 H1 疫苗 候选药物在所有三种 HA 抗原上诱导了最广泛的体液 IgG 反应,而台湾猪 H1 候选药物则引发了最窄的交叉反应免疫。 [10.1186/s12917-026-06097-0]。
  • 仅在 2025-2026 年季节,流感亚型与第 28 天 死亡率 的显着风险无关。在 40 名 甲型H3N2流感病毒 感染者中,33 人(82.5%)属于 K 亚支。系统发育分析显示,没有根据严重程度进行聚类。
  • 更正报告称,原始文章缺少有关致谢部分的信息,其中包括有关研究所用平台和财务支持的详细信息。 [10.1371/journal.ppat.1014784]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • SAM-enabled SEM revealed a distinct topographical 'wavy' pattern on H3N2 virus particle surfaces, interpreted as a preparation-induced artifact from critical point drying shrinkage.
  • Sequence analysis identified 18 distinct amino acid substitutions in the NA gene among the 14 isolates.
  • Results In vivo evaluation demonstrated that the swine-to-human H1 vaccine candidate induced the broadest humoral IgG response across all three HA antigens, whereas the Taiwan porcine H1 candidate elicited the narrowest cross-reactive immunity.
  • In the 2025-2026 season alone, influenza subtype was not associated with a significant risk of day-28 mortality. Among the 40 people with A(H3N2) infection, 33 (82.5%) belonged to subclade K.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期季节性流感病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 该报告指出,流感活动有所增加,且在低水平传播,流感样疾病的急诊部门就诊人数超过季节性预期水平。同时,报告还提到流感确诊的急性呼吸道感染病例数较前一周有所上升。 [news-197ad873751ae63d]。
  • 一项观察性研究调查了2023年11月至2024年12月期间在伊朗设拉子住院患者中的流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 分离株,揭示了基因组变异但未发现主要抗病毒药物耐药性(N1)。 [news-688bfc4aa33ec953]。
  • 全国流感检测阳性率超过10%,主要流行的病毒为流感A型(甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09和甲型H3N2流感病毒)以及流感B型(Victoria和Yamagata谱系)。CDC报告称流感活动和住院病例增加,初步评估显示病情较轻。
  • 文章报道了孕期流感的风险,包括严重症状、并发症以及疫苗接种和及时就医的重要性。 [news-a639bff5ac302078]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Seasonal influenza viruses news developments.
  • The report indicates that influenza activity increased and is circulating at low levels, with ED attendances for influenza-like-illness above seasonally expected levels.
  • An observational study investigated influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 isolates from hospitalized patients in Shiraz, Iran, between November 2023 and December 2024, revealing genomic variability but no major antiviral resistance mutations (N1).
  • Flu test positivity exceeded 10% nationally, with influenza A (H1N1pdm09 and H3N2) and influenza B (Victoria and Yamagata lineages) being the dominant viruses.
  • The article reports on the risks of influenza during pregnancy, including severe symptoms, complications, and the importance of vaccination and medical attention. [news-a639bff5ac302078].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Npj viruses  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-11  |  DOI: 10.1038/s44298-026-00257-7  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 4, 1, 57
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

禽类和人类甲型流感病毒通过粘液凝胶网络的体外渗透。

Penetration of avian and human influenza A viruses through mucus gel networks in vitro.
作者： Maximilian Rohde, Fiona Riegel, Ege Duran, Cosmin Stefan Butnarasu, Daniel Christian Lauster, Christian Sieben, Maximilian Busch
摘要中文翻译摘要 甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 由于其连续的 进化 和人畜共患的 传播 仍然是一个公共卫生问题。呼吸道粘液是吸入病毒遇到的第一个细胞外屏障,但研究流感病毒通过粘液的传播和转移病毒群体的感染性的标准化方法仍然有限。在这里,我们建立了体外病毒-粘液渗透测定来分析 IAV 与标准化含粘蛋白基质的相互作用。我们使用在气液界面条件下培养的人 Calu-3 气道上皮细胞的粘液和牛颌下粘蛋白作为替代基质。通过总蛋白对制剂进行标准化,并使用生化和生物物理方法进行表征。我们将人类季节性 H1N1pdm 病毒与高致病性禽 H5N1 病毒进行了比较。通过 RT-qPCR 对病毒 基因组 转移进行定量,同时评估易感细胞中保留的感染性。与对照溶液相比,两种含有粘蛋白的基质都限制了病毒的通过。病毒 RNA 转移和保留感染性因基质和病毒而异,但在任一基质内均未检测到 H1N1pdm 和 H5N1 之间存在统计学上的显着差异。这些结果共同建立了一个框架,将粘液表征与病毒传播和感染性的补充测量相结合。该方法为在受控条件下研究流感病毒-粘液相互作用提供了基础,并且可以扩展到其他 IAV 毒株和生理相关的粘液来源。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 因其连续的 进化 和人畜共患的 传播 仍然是一个公共卫生问题。该研究旨在解决缺乏标准化方法来研究流感病毒通过粘液传播和转移病毒群体的传染性的问题。
设计与对象
该研究使用人 Calu-3 气道上皮细胞的粘液和牛颌下粘蛋白作为替代基质,建立了体外病毒粘液渗透测定。它将人类季节性 H1N1pdm 病毒与高致病性禽 H5N1 病毒进行了比较。
核心方法
通过总蛋白对制剂进行标准化,并使用生化和生物物理方法进行表征。通过 RT-qPCR 对病毒 基因组 转移进行定量,同时评估易感细胞中保留的感染性。
主要结果
与对照溶液相比,两种含有粘蛋白的基质都限制了病毒的通过。病毒 RNA 转移和保留感染性因基质和病毒而异,但在任一基质内均未检测到 H1N1pdm 和 H5N1 之间存在统计学上的显着差异。
解释与创新
这些结果建立了一个将粘液特征与病毒转移和感染性的补充测量相结合的框架。该方法为在受控条件下研究流感病毒与粘液相互作用提供了基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改进的 监测 和流感病毒诊断方法的开发提供信息,并指导通过粘液针对病毒 传播 的干预措施的设计。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于体外模型,可能无法完全代表体内条件。由于受控实验设置和缺乏临床数据,证据强度中等。

Penetration of avian and human influenza A viruses through mucus gel networks in vitro.

Authors: Maximilian Rohde, Fiona Riegel, Ege Duran, Cosmin Stefan Butnarasu, Daniel Christian Lauster, Christian Sieben, Maximilian Busch
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Influenza A viruses (IAVs) remain a public-health concern because of their continuous evolution and zoonotic transmission. Respiratory mucus is the first extracellular barrier encountered by inhaled viruses, yet standardized approaches for studying influenza virus passage through mucus and the infectivity of transferred virus populations remain limited. Here, we established an in vitro virus–mucus penetration assay to analyze IAV interactions with standardized mucin-containing matrices. We used mucus from human Calu-3 airway epithelial cells cultured under air–liquid interface conditions and bovine submaxillary mucin as surrogate matrices. Preparations were normalized by total protein and characterized using biochemical and biophysical methods. We compared a human seasonal H1N1pdm virus with a highly-pathogenic avian H5N1 virus. Viral genome transfer was quantified by RT-qPCR, while retained infectivity was assessed in susceptible cells. Both mucin-containing matrices restricted viral passage compared with control solutions. Viral RNA transfer and retained infectivity varied by matrix and virus, although no statistically significant difference between H1N1pdm and H5N1 was detected within either matrix. Together, these results establish a framework integrating mucus characterization with complementary measurements of viral transfer and infectivity. This approach provides a basis for investigating influenza virus–mucus interactions under controlled conditions and can be extended to other IAV strains and physiologically relevant mucus sources.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A viruses (IAVs) remain a public-health concern because of their continuous evolution and zoonotic transmission. The study aimed to address the lack of standardized approaches for studying influenza virus passage through mucus and the infectivity of transferred virus populations.
Design and population
The study established an in vitro virus–mucus penetration assay using mucus from human Calu-3 airway epithelial cells and bovine submaxillary mucin as surrogate matrices. It compared a human seasonal H1N1pdm virus with a highly-pathogenic avian H5N1 virus.
Core methods
Preparations were normalized by total protein and characterized using biochemical and biophysical methods. Viral genome transfer was quantified by RT-qPCR, while retained infectivity was assessed in susceptible cells.
Main results
Both mucin-containing matrices restricted viral passage compared with control solutions. Viral RNA transfer and retained infectivity varied by matrix and virus, although no statistically significant difference between H1N1pdm and H5N1 was detected within either matrix.
Interpretation and novelty
These results establish a framework integrating mucus characterization with complementary measurements of viral transfer and infectivity. The approach provides a basis for investigating influenza virus–mucus interactions under controlled conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of improved surveillance and diagnostic methods for influenza viruses, as well as guide the design of interventions targeting viral transmission through mucus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on in vitro models, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the controlled experimental setting and the absence of clinical data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

以流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 分支的出现为标志,2025-2026 年季节期间需要入住 ICU 的流感患者:法国前瞻性全国 队列研究 研究。

People With Influenza Requiring ICU Admission During the 2025-2026 Season, Marked by the Emergence of Influenza A(H3N2) Subclade K: A French Prospective Nationwide Cohort Study.
作者： Nicolas de Prost, Pierre Bay, Marie Le Goff, Sébastien Préau, Aurélie Guigon, François M Beloncle, Caroline Lefeuvre, Anaïs Dartevel, Sylvie Larrat, Rémi Coudroy
摘要中文翻译背景:在流感甲型H3N2流感病毒亚系K出现的同时,报告了强烈的流感活动。尽管流行病学研究在公共卫生影响方面报告了异质性结果,但关于其对需要重症监护病房(ICU)入院人群临床表型的影响的数据仍然缺乏。方法:一项前瞻性、多中心、观察性SEVARVIR 队列研究研究,包括在2024-2025和2025-2026季节期间在42个法国ICU中接受实验室确诊流感感染的患者。主要终点是第28天死亡率。结果:在2024-2025年(n = 360)和2025-2026年(n = 366)期间,共有726名患者因流感入院。在2025-2026年,有265名患者的流感亚型可供分析;其中49%(n = 129)为甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09亚型感染,51%(n = 136)为甲型H3N2流感病毒亚型感染。在40名接受全基因组测序的甲型H3N2流感病毒感染患者中,33名(82.5%)属于亚系K。在2025-2026年季节的多变量Cox分析中,年龄(调整后风险比,aHR = 1.04 [1.01;1.09];P = .015)、临床脆弱性量表(aHR = 1.59 [1.28;1.98];P < .001)以及序贯器官衰竭评估评分(aHR = 1.15 [1.05;1.26];P = .003)与死亡风险增加相关。在2024-2025/2025-2026年季节或仅2025-2026年季节中,流感亚型与第28天死亡率的显著风险无关联。我们的ICU 队列研究的系统发育分析,结合了600个来自同期社区获得性流感病例的上下文序列,未显示根据严重程度的聚集。结论:在2025-2026年季节,尽管出现了流感甲型H3N2流感病毒亚系K,但没有证据表明重症流感患者死亡风险增加。试验注册号:NCT05162508。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
据报道,随着流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 亚支的出现,流感活动也随之加剧。该研究旨在调查其对需要入住 ICU 的患者临床表型的影响。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性、多中心、观察性 SEVARVIR 队列研究 研究在 42 个法国 ICU 中进行,其中包括 2024-2025 年和 2025-2026 年季节期间因实验室确诊流感感染入院的患者。总计726人,其中2024-2025赛季360人,2025-2026赛季366人。
核心方法
该研究包括第 28 天 死亡率 的主要终点。对 40 名 甲型H3N2流感病毒 感染者进行了全 基因组 测序,并使用来自社区获得性流感病例的 600 个背景序列进行了系统发育分析。
主要结果
仅在 2025-2026 年季节,流感亚型与第 28 天 死亡率 的显着风险无关。在 40 名 甲型H3N2流感病毒 感染者中,33 人(82.5%)属于 K 亚支。系统发育分析显示,没有根据严重程度进行聚类。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,尽管出现了流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 分支,但没有证据表明 2025-2026 年季节期间流感重症患者的死亡风险增加。这一发现很新颖,因为它直接解决了 K 分支对 ICU 结局的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,流感甲型H3N2流感病毒 K亚支的出现并没有显着影响危重患者的死亡率率,这可能为未来的监测和季节性流感的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,不包括对照组,这可能会限制证据的强度。然而,全 基因组 测序和系统发育分析的使用提供了一种稳健的方法学方法。

People With Influenza Requiring ICU Admission During the 2025-2026 Season, Marked by the Emergence of Influenza A(H3N2) Subclade K: A French Prospective Nationwide Cohort Study.

Authors: Nicolas de Prost, Pierre Bay, Marie Le Goff, Sébastien Préau, Aurélie Guigon, François M Beloncle, Caroline Lefeuvre, Anaïs Dartevel, Sylvie Larrat, Rémi Coudroy
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Intense influenza activity has been reported alongside the emergence of influenza A(H3N2) subclade K. While epidemiological studies have reported heterogeneous findings regarding public health impact, data remain lacking on its impact on clinical phenotype of people requiring intensive care unit (ICU) admission. METHODS: Prospective, multicenter, observational SEVARVIR cohort study including people admitted in 42 French ICUs with laboratory-confirmed influenza infection during the 2024-2025 and 2025-2026 seasons. Primary end point was day-28 mortality. RESULTS: Seven hundred twenty-six people were admitted for influenza in 2024-2025 (n = 360) and 2025-2026 (n = 366). In 2025-2026, influenza subtype was available for 265 people; 49% (n = 129) had A(H1N1)pdm09 and 51% (n = 136) had A(H3N2) subtype infection. Among the 40 people with A(H3N2) infection for whom whole-genome sequencing was available, 33 (82.5%) belonged to subclade K. In multivariable Cox analysis of the 2025-2026 season, age (adjusted hazard ratio, aHR = 1.04 [1.01;1.09]; P = .015), Clinical Frailty Scale (aHR = 1.59 [1.28;1.98]; P < .001), and sequential organ failure assessment score (aHR = 1.15 [1.05;1.26]; P = .003) were associated with an increased risk of death. In the 2024-2025/2025-2026 seasons or 2025-2026 season alone, influenza subtype was not associated with a significant risk of day-28 mortality. Phylogenetic analyses from our ICU cohort together with 600 contextual sequences from community-acquired influenza cases collected during the period showed no clustering according to severity. CONCLUSIONS: There was no evidence of an increased risk of death among critically ill people with influenza during the 2025-2026 season, despite the emergence of influenza A(H3N2) subclade K. TRIAL REGISTRATION: NCT05162508.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Intense influenza activity has been reported alongside the emergence of influenza A(H3N2) subclade K. The study aimed to investigate its impact on the clinical phenotype of people requiring ICU admission.
Design and population
A prospective, multicenter, observational SEVARVIR cohort study was conducted in 42 French ICUs, including people admitted with laboratory-confirmed influenza infection during the 2024-2025 and 2025-2026 seasons. A total of 726 people were included, with 360 in the 2024-2025 season and 366 in the 2025-2026 season.
Core methods
The study included a primary endpoint of day-28 mortality. Whole-genome sequencing was performed on 40 people with A(H3N2) infection, and phylogenetic analyses were conducted using 600 contextual sequences from community-acquired influenza cases.
Main results
In the 2025-2026 season alone, influenza subtype was not associated with a significant risk of day-28 mortality. Among the 40 people with A(H3N2) infection, 33 (82.5%) belonged to subclade K. Phylogenetic analyses showed no clustering according to severity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that there was no evidence of an increased risk of death among critically ill people with influenza during the 2025-2026 season, despite the emergence of influenza A(H3N2) subclade K. This finding is novel as it directly addresses the impact of subclade K on ICU outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the emergence of influenza A(H3N2) subclade K did not significantly affect mortality rates in critically ill patients, which may inform future surveillance and public health strategies for seasonal influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was observational and did not include a control group, which may limit the strength of the evidence. However, the use of whole-genome sequencing and phylogenetic analyses provides a robust methodological approach.

Journal: Microscopy (Oxford, England)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-13  |  DOI: 10.1093/jmicro/dfag042  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, dfag042
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

基质粘附法 (SAM):甲型流感病毒 (H3N2) 形态和消毒剂引起的变化的无超速离心电子显微镜。

Substrate Adhesion Method (SAM): Ultracentrifugation-Free Electron Microscopy of Influenza A virus (H3N2) Morphology and Disinfectant-Induced Changes.
作者： Tomoki Nishida, Eri Nakajima, Satoshi Seino, Yasuo Imoto
摘要中文翻译摘要 电子显微镜 (EM) 对于解析病毒超微结构至关重要,但用于样品浓缩的传统超速离心可能会导致结构伪影,特别是在甲型流感病毒等包膜病毒中。为了克服这个问题,我们开发并验证了基材粘附方法 (SAM)。这种简单的技术利用甲型流感病毒 (H3N2) 在感染过程中对细胞培养基质的自然粘附,绕过超速离心,无需事先浓缩即可对子代病毒颗粒进行有效的多模式 EM(SEM、TEM、STEM)可视化。启用 SAM 的 SEM 揭示了 H3N2 病毒颗粒表面明显的地形“波状”图案,这被解释为由临界点干燥 (CPD) 收缩引起的制剂诱导伪影。超高压电子断层扫描证实,含有尖峰的包膜结构有助于观察到这种形态。因此,SAM 辅助的 SEM 提供了宽视场形态学见解,补充了 TEM 的高分辨率内部结构分析。受感染细胞显着增强了病毒颗粒通过 SAM 与基质的粘附,表明细胞相关因子在这种固定中发挥着关键作用。此外,SAM 与负染色的兼容性允许通过不同的包膜变形(例如破坏和粗糙化)直接可视化乙醇诱导的 H3N2 病毒灭活。这一功能使 SAM 成为直接形态学评估消毒剂功效、补充感染性测定的宝贵工具。总之,SAM 为 H3N2 病毒的详细形态学研究和 抗病毒药物 药物评估提供了一种简单、快速、减少伪影的方法,为病毒学 EM 开辟了新的视角。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种方法来克服甲型流感病毒(H3N2)电子显微镜中超速离心引起的结构伪影,这是解决病毒超微结构的关键问题。
设计与对象
该研究使用电子显微镜技术(包括 SEM、TEM 和 STEM)设计并验证了甲型流感病毒 (H3N2) 可视化的基质粘附方法 (SAM)。
核心方法
SAM 通过利用 H3N2 病毒颗粒在感染过程中与细胞培养基质的自然粘附,绕过超速离心,无需事先浓缩即可实现有效的多模式 EM 可视化。
主要结果
启用 SAM 的 SEM 揭示了 H3N2 病毒颗粒表面明显的“波状”形貌图案,这被解释为临界点干燥收缩导致的制剂诱导伪影。超高压电子断层扫描证实,含有尖峰的包膜结构有助于观察到这种形态。SAM 与负染色的兼容性允许通过不同的包膜变形直接观察乙醇诱导的 H3N2 病毒灭活。
解释与创新
SAM 为 H3N2 病毒的详细形态学研究和 抗病毒药物 制剂评估提供了一种简单、快速、减少伪影的方法,提供了广泛的形态学见解,补充了 TEM 的高分辨率内部结构分析。
科研与公卫意义
SAM 是一种有价值的工具,可用于直接形态学评估消毒剂功效、补充感染性测定并开辟病毒学 EM 的新视角。
局限与证据强度
受感染细胞显着增强了病毒颗粒通过 SAM 与基质的粘附,表明细胞相关因子在这种固定中发挥着关键作用。然而,该研究并未报告 SAM 对其他流感病毒亚型或物种的普遍性。

Substrate Adhesion Method (SAM): Ultracentrifugation-Free Electron Microscopy of Influenza A virus (H3N2) Morphology and Disinfectant-Induced Changes.

Authors: Tomoki Nishida, Eri Nakajima, Satoshi Seino, Yasuo Imoto
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Electron microscopy (EM) is crucial for resolving viral ultrastructure, but conventional ultracentrifugation used for sample concentration can induce structural artifacts, particularly in enveloped viruses like influenza A virus. To overcome this, we developed and validated the Substrate Adhesion Method (SAM). This simple technique bypasses ultracentrifugation by utilizing the natural adhesion of influenza A virus (H3N2) to cell culture substrates during infection, enabling effective multimodal EM (SEM, TEM, STEM) visualization of progeny virus particles without prior concentration. SAM-enabled SEM revealed a distinct topographical "wavy" pattern on H3N2 virus particle surfaces, which is interpreted as a preparation-induced artifact arising from critical point drying (CPD) shrinkage. Ultra-high-voltage electron tomography confirmed that spike-containing envelope structures contribute to this observed morphology. Thus, SAM-facilitated SEM offers wide-field morphological insights complementing TEM's high-resolution internal structural analysis. Virus particle adhesion to the substrate via SAM was significantly enhanced by infected cells, suggesting a key role for cell-associated factors in this immobilization. Furthermore, SAM's compatibility with negative staining allowed direct visualization of ethanol-induced H3N2 virus inactivation via distinct envelope deformations such as disruption and roughening. This feature makes SAM a valuable tool for direct morphological assessment of disinfectant efficacy, complementing infectivity assays. In conclusion, SAM provides a simple, rapid, artifact-mitigating approach for detailed morphological studies of H3N2 virus and antiviral agent evaluation, opening new perspectives in virological EM.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method to overcome the structural artifacts induced by ultracentrifugation in electron microscopy of influenza A virus (H3N2), a critical issue for resolving viral ultrastructure.
Design and population
The study designed and validated the Substrate Adhesion Method (SAM) for influenza A virus (H3N2) visualization using electron microscopy techniques, including SEM, TEM, and STEM.
Core methods
SAM bypasses ultracentrifugation by utilizing the natural adhesion of H3N2 virus particles to cell culture substrates during infection, enabling effective multimodal EM visualization without prior concentration.
Main results
SAM-enabled SEM revealed a distinct topographical 'wavy' pattern on H3N2 virus particle surfaces, interpreted as a preparation-induced artifact from critical point drying shrinkage. Ultra-high-voltage electron tomography confirmed that spike-containing envelope structures contribute to this observed morphology. SAM's compatibility with negative staining allowed direct visualization of ethanol-induced H3N2 virus inactivation via distinct envelope deformations.
Interpretation and novelty
SAM provides a simple, rapid, artifact-mitigating approach for detailed morphological studies of H3N2 virus and antiviral agent evaluation, offering wide-field morphological insights complementing TEM's high-resolution internal structural analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
SAM is a valuable tool for direct morphological assessment of disinfectant efficacy, complementing infectivity assays, and opening new perspectives in virological EM.
Limitations and evidence strength
Virus particle adhesion to the substrate via SAM was significantly enhanced by infected cells, suggesting a key role for cell-associated factors in this immobilization. However, the study does not report on the generalizability of SAM to other influenza virus subtypes or species.

2010 年和 2013 年 发病率 与 A 型 H1N1 和 B 型维多利亚流感病毒株相关的发作性睡病和特发性嗜睡症高峰。

2010 and 2013 Incidence Peaks in Narcolepsy and Idiopathic Hypersomnia Linked to Type A H1N1 and Type B Victoria Influenza Strains.
作者： Zhongxing Zhang, Jari K. Gool, Pavel Sirotkin, Yves A Dauvilliers, Lucie Barateau, Giuseppe Plazzi, Fabio Pizza, Francesco Biscarini, Karel Sonka, Karolina Galuskova
摘要中文翻译摘要 2010 年,全球报告 1 型发作性睡病 (NT1) 发病率 发病率增加,与 2009-2010 年 H1N1 流感大流行和 Pandemrix 疫苗接种 有关。2013 年在欧洲观察到儿童特有的 NT1 峰值,进一步引发了人们对 NT1 与流感感染之间关系的疑问。本研究分析了欧洲中枢性嗜睡症 (CDH) 的年度 发病率 模式与流感严重程度的关系。1995 年至 2019 年,对来自 8 个欧洲中心的所有 NT1(N = 981)以及 2 型发作性睡病 (NT2) 和特发性嗜睡症 (IH) (N = 545) 病例的组合组进行了研究。应用线性混合模型和 Spearman 相关性来研究 CDH 发病率 与之前流感季节(包括 H1N1、H3N2)严重程度之间的关系,以及荷兰,维多利亚和山形 B 型流感。NT1 发病率 中的峰值出现于 2010 年和 2013 年,而 NT2 和 IH 中则发现了 2010 年儿童特异性峰值。两组均与 H1N1 流感严重程度呈显着正相关,与 H3N2 呈负相关。NT1 还与荷兰的维多利亚 B 型流感显着相关,尤其是儿童。这些发现表明,除了 H1N1 流感之外,维多利亚 B 型流感可能是 NT1 的新触发因素。与 NT1 类似,2010 年儿童的 NT2 和 IH 达到峰值可能表明可能与 2009-2010 年 H1N1 大流行有关的免疫相关病理生理学。需要进一步的免疫学研究来阐明这些关系的复杂性及其对 CDH 的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 2009-2010 年 H1N1 流感大流行和 Pandemrix 疫苗接种 后,季节性流感 病毒与中枢性嗜睡症 发病率 之间的关系,特别是 1 型发作性睡病 (NT1) 和特发性嗜睡症 (IH)。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 1995 年至 2019 年欧洲中枢性嗜睡症的年度 发病率 模式,包括来自八个欧洲中心的所有 NT1 病例 (N = 981) 以及 NT2 和 IH 病例组合组 (N = 545)。
核心方法
使用线性混合模型和 Spearman 相关性来检查 CDH 发病率 与之前流感季节的严重程度之间的关系,特别是 H1N1、H3N2,以及荷兰的 B 型流感维多利亚和山形。
主要结果
NT1 发病率 中的峰在 2010 年和 2013 年观察到,而 2010 年儿童特异性峰在 NT2 和 IH 中被识别。两组均与 H1N1 流感严重程度呈显着正相关,与 H3N2 呈负相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,维多利亚 B 型流感可能是 NT1 的新触发因素,特别是在儿童中,超出了之前已知的与 H1N1 的关联。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了 季节性流感 病毒在嗜睡症和特发性嗜睡症病理生理学中的潜在作用,为未来的 监测 和研究工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于观察数据,没有建立因果关系,限制了证据的强度。然而,统计模型的使用为观察到的关联提供了中等程度的置信度。

2010 and 2013 Incidence Peaks in Narcolepsy and Idiopathic Hypersomnia Linked to Type A H1N1 and Type B Victoria Influenza Strains.

Authors: Zhongxing Zhang, Jari K. Gool, Pavel Sirotkin, Yves A Dauvilliers, Lucie Barateau, Giuseppe Plazzi, Fabio Pizza, Francesco Biscarini, Karel Sonka, Karolina Galuskova
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Increased narcolepsy type‐1 (NT1) incidence rates were reported globally in 2010, linked to the H1N1 2009–2010 influenza pandemic and Pandemrix vaccination. A child‐specific NT1 peak was observed in Europe in 2013, further raising questions about the relationship between NT1 and influenza infections. This study analysed annual European incidence patterns of central disorders of hypersomnolence (CDH) in relation to influenza severity. All NT1 ( N = 981) and a combined group of narcolepsy type‐2 (NT2) and idiopathic hypersomnia (IH) ( N = 545) cases from eight European centers were studied from 1995 to 2019. Linear mixed models and Spearman correlations were applied to investigate the relationships between CDH incidence and the severity of preceding influenza seasons, including H1N1, H3N2, and for the Netherlands, influenza Type B Victoria and Yamagata. Peaks in NT1 incidence were present in 2010 and 2013, while a 2010 child‐specific peak was identified in NT2 and IH. Both groups showed a significantly positive correlation with H1N1 influenza severity and a negative correlation with H3N2. NT1 was also significantly associated with influenza Type B Victoria in the Netherlands, particularly in children. These findings suggest that, beyond H1N1, influenza Type B Victoria may be a novel trigger for NT1. Similar to NT1, the 2010 children peak in NT2 and IH may indicate possible immune‐related pathophysiologies linked to the 2009–2010 H1N1 pandemic. Further immunological studies are warranted to unravel the complexities of these relationships and their implications for CDH.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the relationship between seasonal influenza viruses and the incidence of central disorders of hypersomnolence, particularly narcolepsy type-1 (NT1) and idiopathic hypersomnia (IH), following the 2009–2010 H1N1 pandemic and the Pandemrix vaccination.
Design and population
The study analyzed annual European incidence patterns of central disorders of hypersomnolence from 1995 to 2019, including all NT1 cases (N = 981) and a combined group of NT2 and IH cases (N = 545) from eight European centers.
Core methods
Linear mixed models and Spearman correlations were used to examine the relationship between CDH incidence and the severity of preceding influenza seasons, specifically H1N1, H3N2, and for the Netherlands, influenza Type B Victoria and Yamagata.
Main results
Peaks in NT1 incidence were observed in 2010 and 2013, while a 2010 child-specific peak was identified in NT2 and IH. Both groups showed a significantly positive correlation with H1N1 influenza severity and a negative correlation with H3N2.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that influenza Type B Victoria may be a novel trigger for NT1, particularly in children, beyond the previously known association with H1N1.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the potential role of seasonal influenza viruses in the pathophysiology of narcolepsy and idiopathic hypersomnia, informing future surveillance and research efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on observational data and does not establish causality, limiting the strength of the evidence. However, the use of statistical models provides a moderate level of confidence in the observed associations.

从伊朗设拉子住院患者分离的流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒的神经氨酸酶基因和 抗病毒药物 易感性的基因组变异(2023-2024)

Genomic variability in the neuraminidase gene and antiviral susceptibility of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses isolated from hospitalized patients in Shiraz, Iran (2023–2024)
作者： Mohammad Javad Shahbazi Nezhad, Neda Pirbonyeh, Mojtaba Mortazavi, Fahime Edalat, Amirreza Radfar, Jamal Sarvari, Afagh Moattari
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 2023 年至 2024 年期间从伊朗设拉子住院患者中分离出的流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒神经氨酸酶基因的基因组变异,以评估对 抗病毒药物 易感性和病毒 进化 的潜在影响。
设计与对象
2023年11月至2024年12月期间进行了一项横断面研究,收集了伊朗设拉子急性呼吸道感染患者的300份呼吸道样本,并分析了14份流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09阳性样本。
核心方法
使用实时 RT-PCR 对呼吸道样本进行流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 筛查。阳性样本进行全长NA基因测序、氨基酸取代分析、系统发育分析、结构建模以及与奥司他韦和扎那米韦的分子对接。
主要结果
Sequence analysis identified 18 distinct amino acid substitutions in the NA gene among the 14 isolates. The most frequent substitutions were V241I and N369K (13/14, 92.9%), followed by N200S (4/14, 28.6%) and V394I (2/14, 14.3%).
解释与创新
研究结果表明,分析的流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 分离株表现出 NA 基因的遗传变异性,但缺乏已确定的神经氨酸酶抑制剂耐药性相关取代,这凸显了对持续分子 监测 的需求。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒的 进化 基因及其对神经氨酸酶抑制剂的潜在敏感性的深入了解,这对于监测 抗病毒药物 耐药性和为公共卫生策略提供信息至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,仅限于 14 个分离株,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它基于序列分析和分子对接,没有临床结果数据。

Genomic variability in the neuraminidase gene and antiviral susceptibility of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses isolated from hospitalized patients in Shiraz, Iran (2023–2024)

Authors: Mohammad Javad Shahbazi Nezhad, Neda Pirbonyeh, Mojtaba Mortazavi, Fahime Edalat, Amirreza Radfar, Jamal Sarvari, Afagh Moattari
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The 2009 influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 pandemic caused substantial global morbidity and mortality. Mutations in the neuraminidase (NA) gene may influence viral evolution, immune evasion, and susceptibility to neuraminidase inhibitors, highlighting the importance of continuous molecular surveillance. Methods Between November 2023 and December 2024, 300 respiratory samples were collected from patients with acute respiratory infections in Shiraz, Iran, and screened for influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 by real-time RT-PCR. Fourteen positive samples underwent full-length NA gene sequencing, amino acid substitution analysis, phylogenetic analysis, structural modeling, and molecular docking with oseltamivir and zanamivir. Phylogenetic comparison was performed using the historical pandemic reference strain A/California/07/2009 (NC_026434.1) to identify neuraminidase sequence divergence relative to the founding A(H1N1)pdm09 lineage. Results Sequence analysis identified 18 distinct amino acid substitutions in the NA gene among the 14 isolates. The most frequent substitutions were V241I and N369K (13/14, 92.9%), followed by N200S (4/14, 28.6%) and V394I (2/14, 14.3%). V241I and N369K are consistent with previously reported lineage-associated permissive substitutions in circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses, whereas N386K has been associated with alteration of an N-linked glycosylation site. Screening of the NA sequences for established WHO-recognized neuraminidase inhibitor resistance-associated substitutions identified no major known resistance markers, including H275Y and I223V. Molecular docking suggested preserved predicted binding of oseltamivir and zanamivir to neuraminidase. Conclusion Influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses isolated from hospitalized patients in Shiraz during 2023–2024 exhibited genetic variability in the neuraminidase gene while lacking established neuraminidase inhibitor resistance-associated substitutions. These findings provide sequence-based evidence consistent with the absence of established resistance markers among the analyzed isolates and emphasize the importance of continued molecular surveillance to monitor the emergence of clinically relevant NA mutations. Clinical Trial Registration Not applicable, as this study is observational and does not involve a clinical trial.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate genomic variability in the neuraminidase gene of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses isolated from hospitalized patients in Shiraz, Iran, during 2023–2024, to assess potential implications for antiviral susceptibility and viral evolution.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted between November 2023 and December 2024, collecting 300 respiratory samples from patients with acute respiratory infections in Shiraz, Iran, and analyzing 14 positive samples for influenza A(H1N1)pdm09.
Core methods
Respiratory samples were screened for influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 using real-time RT-PCR. Positive samples underwent full-length NA gene sequencing, amino acid substitution analysis, phylogenetic analysis, structural modeling, and molecular docking with oseltamivir and zanamivir.
Main results
Sequence analysis identified 18 distinct amino acid substitutions in the NA gene among the 14 isolates. The most frequent substitutions were V241I and N369K (13/14, 92.9%), followed by N200S (4/14, 28.6%) and V394I (2/14, 14.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the analyzed influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 isolates exhibited genetic variability in the NA gene but lacked established neuraminidase inhibitor resistance-associated substitutions, highlighting the need for continued molecular surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the genetic evolution of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses and their potential susceptibility to neuraminidase inhibitors, which is critical for monitoring antiviral resistance and informing public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and limited to 14 isolates, which may restrict generalizability. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on sequence analysis and molecular docking without clinical outcome data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

绝对颗粒定量显示,甲型流感病毒的感染单位是颗粒群,而不是单个病毒粒子

The infectious unit of influenza A virus is a population of particles, not a single virion, as shown by absolute particle quantification
作者： Yuu Kawahara, Kenji Tamao, Yoichiro Fujioka, Seiya Yamayoshi, Yusuke Ohba, Yoshihiro Kawaoka, Hiroyuki Noji, Kazuhito V. Tabata
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 群体的特点是非感染性病毒体 患病率 较高,颗粒与噬菌斑形成单位 (PFU) 的比率通常为 10 至 1,000。虽然“多重再激活”——不完整病毒 基因组 (IVG) 通过共感染建立生产性感染的过程——长期以来一直被假设,但其定量性质仍然难以捉摸。在这项研究中,我们利用数字流感检测来实现 IAV 颗粒的绝对定量,从而使我们能够定义基于颗粒的感染复数 (pMOI) 进行精确评估。我们将这种方法与使用微区室容器和基于机器学习的自动细胞分类的单细胞感染测定相结合,以准确评估各种 pMOI 的感染结果。我们对 A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (H1N1) 的分析显示,计数与噬菌斑形成单位 (PFU) 的比率约为 400,这意味着只有一小部分颗粒具有传染性。值得注意的是,单细胞感染实验中的感染率遵循 S 形曲线,并在 pMOI 仅 5 至 10 时达到饱和。这表明,在高 pMOI 条件下,生产性感染主要由不完整颗粒之间的 基因组 互补驱动,而不是仅由完全感染性病毒粒子驱动。另一方面,基于这些发现的数学模型分析表明,病毒颗粒内八个基因组片段中每一个的功能遵循随机过程,从而产生每个颗粒的功能片段数量的概率分布。尽管 CTPR 值存在 20 倍的变化,但该机制在多种菌株中被证明异常稳健。最终,这些发现表明,不完整颗粒不仅仅是生物噪音,而且是感染过程的积极贡献者,显着增强了 IAV 的感染性和进化灵活性。这项研究将为流感 传播 动态和风险评估提供更深入的了解,特别是在单个呼吸道飞沫中存在多个病毒颗粒的环境中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究旨在解决关于“多重性重新激活”及其定量性质的长期假设,特别是在以非传染性病毒颗粒高 患病率 为特征的甲型流感病毒群体的背景下。
设计与对象
该研究采用数字流感检测和微室化容器的单细胞感染实验进行,重点关注 A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (H1N1) 毒株,以评估各种基于颗粒的感染复数 (pMOI) 值的感染结果。
核心方法
研究人员采用数字流感检测来实现 IAV 颗粒的绝对定量,并将其与使用微区室容器和基于机器学习的自动细胞分类的单细胞感染检测相结合,以评估感染结果。
主要结果
对 A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (H1N1) 病毒株的分析显示,计数与空斑形成单位 (PFU) 的比率约为 400,表明只有一小部分颗粒具有传染性。单细胞实验中的感染率遵循S形曲线,并在pMOI为5至10时达到饱和。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,高 pMOI 条件下的生产性感染主要是由不完整颗粒之间的 基因组 互补驱动的,而不是完全感染性病毒粒子,这为了解不完整颗粒在流感 传播 中的作用提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对流感 传播 动态和风险评估的更精细的了解,特别是在单个呼吸道飞沫中存在多个病毒颗粒的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于单一菌株,这可能会限制普遍性,但该方法是稳健的,并且结果得到了清晰而精确的实验设计的支持。

The infectious unit of influenza A virus is a population of particles, not a single virion, as shown by absolute particle quantification

Authors: Yuu Kawahara, Kenji Tamao, Yoichiro Fujioka, Seiya Yamayoshi, Yusuke Ohba, Yoshihiro Kawaoka, Hiroyuki Noji, Kazuhito V. Tabata
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) populations are characterized by a high prevalence of non-infectious virions, with particle-to-plaque forming unit (PFU) ratios typically ranging from 10 to 1,000. While "multiplicity reactivation" -the process by which incomplete viral genomes (IVGs) establish productive infection through co-infection- has long been hypothesized, its quantitative nature has remained elusive. In this study, we utilized a digital influenza assay to achieve absolute quantification of IAV particles, allowing us to define the particle-based multiplicity of infection (pMOI) for precise evaluation. We combined this approach with a single-cell infection assay using microcompartmentalized containers and machine learning-based automated cell classification to accurately assess infection outcomes across a wide range of pMOIs. Our analysis of the A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (H1N1) revealed a count-to-plaque forming unit (PFU) ratio of approximately 400, meaning only a tiny fraction of particles possesses infectivity. Remarkably, the infection rate in single-cell infection experiments followed a sigmoidal curve and reached saturation at a pMOI of only 5 to 10. This demonstrates that under high-pMOI conditions, productive infection is predominantly driven by genome complementation among incomplete particles rather than solely by fully infectious virions. On the other hand, mathematical model analysis based on these findings suggested that the functionality of each of the eight genomic segments within a virus particle follows a stochastic process, resulting in a probability distribution for the number of functional segments per particle. This mechanism proved exceptionally robust across multiple strains despite a 20-fold variation in CTPR values. Ultimately, these findings indicate that incomplete particles are not merely biological noise but active contributors to the infection process, significantly enhancing the infectivity and evolutionary flexibility of IAV. This study will provide a more refined understanding of influenza transmission dynamics and risk assessment, particularly in environments where multiple virus particles are present within a single respiratory droplet.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the long-standing hypothesis about "multiplicity reactivation" and its quantitative nature, particularly in the context of influenza A virus populations that are characterized by a high prevalence of non-infectious virions.
Design and population
The study was conducted using a digital influenza assay and single-cell infection experiments with microcompartmentalized containers, focusing on the A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (H1N1) strain to evaluate infection outcomes across a wide range of particle-based multiplicity of infection (pMOI) values.
Core methods
The researchers employed a digital influenza assay to achieve absolute quantification of IAV particles and combined this with a single-cell infection assay using microcompartmentalized containers and machine learning-based automated cell classification to assess infection outcomes.
Main results
Analysis of the A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (H1N1) strain revealed a count-to-plaque forming unit (PFU) ratio of approximately 400, indicating that only a small fraction of particles is infectious. The infection rate in single-cell experiments followed a sigmoidal curve and reached saturation at a pMOI of 5 to 10.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that productive infection under high-pMOI conditions is primarily driven by genome complementation among incomplete particles rather than fully infectious virions, offering a novel perspective on the role of incomplete particles in influenza transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a more refined understanding of influenza transmission dynamics and risk assessment, especially in environments where multiple virus particles are present within a single respiratory droplet.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are based on a single strain, which may limit generalizability, but the methodology is robust and the results are supported by a clear and precise experimental design.

Authors: Marie Thirion, Alice J. Stelfox, Florian Chenavier, Héléna Chomat, Catherine Isel, Lily-Lorette Freslon, Eleftherios Zarkadas, Rob W. H. Ruigrok, Nadia Naffakh, Thibaut Crépin

Question and background
The study was a correction to a previous article, addressing missing information in the Acknowledgement section regarding the support and facilities used for the research on the nucleocapsid-like cryo-EM structure of influenza B virus.
Design and population
The study is a correction to the original research on the nucleocapsid-like cryo-EM structure of influenza B virus, published in 2026. It does not involve a new study design or population but focuses on clarifying the supporting infrastructure and funding sources.
Core methods
The correction provides details about the facilities and funding sources used in the original study, including the Grenoble Instruct-ERIC center, the Grenoble Partnership for Structural Biology, and financial support from FRISBI and GRAL.

更新 CDC 甲型 (H3) 流感亚型分析,以改进流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒(包括 K 亚支病毒)的检测

Update to the CDC influenza A(H3) subtyping assay for improved detection of influenza A(H3N2) viruses, including subclade K viruses
作者： Bo Shu, Jimma Liddell, Phili Wong, Eduardo Salinas Duron, Ji Liu, Norman Hassell, Juliana DaSilva, Kai-Hui Wu, Angie Foust, Rebecca J. Kondor
摘要中文翻译摘要 流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒在全球传播并引起呼吸道疾病。FDA 批准的 CDC 人类流感病毒实时 RT-PCR 甲型流感 (H3/H1pdm09) 亚型分析用于对 季节性流感 A 病毒进行亚型分析。然而,H3 反向引物区域中出现的 突变 引起了人们对当代流行病毒检测灵敏度降低的担忧。我们通过重新设计反向引物以针对血凝素基因的更保守区域,并对正向引物和探针进行细微调整,更新了 H3 测定。使用培养的流感病毒、合成 RNA 和非流感呼吸道病原体组进行分析敏感性、包容性和排他性评估。使用季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 残留呼吸道标本评估临床表现。更新后的测定 (H3_v2) 证明了分析灵敏度与初始 H3 测定相当或有所提高,检测限约为 10 1.7 –10 2.1 EID 50 /mL,每个反应有 5 个 RNA 拷贝。对于反向引物中含有 A21G 和 T23G、T23C 或 T23A 双错配的病毒,观察到性能有所改善,初始 H3 和 H3_v2 测定之间的 Ct 值发生可测量的变化。该检测结果与 139 份临床样本的预期结果 100% 一致,并且与其他流感亚型或其他呼吸道病原体没有交叉反应。这些发现表明,更新的 H3_v2 检测提高了对当代和新兴季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的敏感性,同时保持了高特异性。此更新可确保持续可靠地检测季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒,包括基因漂移的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒,例如最近出现的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 亚支病毒,并支持全球流感 监测 和诊断准备。重要性 流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的准确检测和分型对于临床诊断、暴发 应对和全球流感 监测 至关重要。突变 的出现和固定导致 FDA 批准的 CDC A(H3) 实时 RT-PCR 检测中引物错配,引起了人们对当代流行的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒敏感性降低的担忧。为了解决这个问题,我们重新设计了反向引物并优化了其他检测组件,以改善对当前流行的季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的检测。更新后的 H3_v2 检测恢复并增强了灵敏度,包括对具有关键反向引物错配的病毒的灵敏度,同时保持了高特异性并与临床标本完全一致。此更新确保可靠检测 季节性流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 变种病毒,包括最近出现的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 亚支病毒,并加强国内和国际的诊断准备和 监测 能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由于 H3 反向引物区域中出现的 突变 导致 CDC 甲型(H3)流感亚型分析对当代流行的流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的敏感性降低的担忧。
设计与对象
该研究涉及通过重新设计反向引物和优化其他检测成分来更新 H3 检测,以改善对当前流行的季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的检测。使用培养的流感病毒、合成 RNA 和非流感呼吸道病原体组进行评估。
核心方法
使用培养的流感病毒、合成 RNA 和非流感呼吸道病原体组评估分析敏感性、包容性和排他性。使用季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 残留呼吸道标本评估临床表现。
主要结果
更新后的测定 (H3_v2) 证明了分析灵敏度与初始 H3 测定相当或有所提高,检测限约为 10^1.7 –10^2.1 EID50/mL,每个反应有 5 个 RNA 拷贝。该检测结果与 139 份临床样本的预期结果 100% 一致,并且与其他流感亚型或呼吸道病原体没有交叉反应。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,更新的 H3_v2 检测提高了对当代和新兴季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的敏感性,同时保持高特异性,这是诊断工具的新颖增强。
科研与公卫意义
更新后的检测方法可确保可靠检测 季节性流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 及其变种病毒,包括最近出现的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 亚支病毒,从而加强诊断准备和全球流感 监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于使用培养病毒和临床标本评估更新的检测方法,证据充分,因为它表明与预期结果 100% 一致,并且与其他病原体没有交叉反应。

Update to the CDC influenza A(H3) subtyping assay for improved detection of influenza A(H3N2) viruses, including subclade K viruses

Authors: Bo Shu, Jimma Liddell, Phili Wong, Eduardo Salinas Duron, Ji Liu, Norman Hassell, Juliana DaSilva, Kai-Hui Wu, Angie Foust, Rebecca J. Kondor
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Influenza A(H3N2) viruses circulate globally and cause respiratory illness. The FDA-cleared CDC Human Influenza Virus Real-Time RT-PCR Influenza A (H3/H1pdm09) Subtyping assay is used to subtype seasonal influenza A viruses. However, emerging mutations in the H3 reverse primer region raised concerns about reduced assay sensitivity for contemporary circulating viruses. We updated the H3 assay by redesigning the reverse primer to target a more conserved region of the hemagglutinin gene, and incorporating minor adjustments into the forward primer and probe. Analytical sensitivity, inclusivity, and exclusivity evaluations were performed using cultured influenza viruses, synthetic RNA, and panels of non-influenza respiratory pathogens. Clinical performance was evaluated using seasonal A(H3N2) residual respiratory specimens. The updated assay (H3_v2) demonstrated analytical sensitivity comparable to or improved over the initial H3 assay, with a limit of detection of approximately 10 1.7 –10 2.1 EID 50 /mL and five copies of RNA per reaction. Improved performance was observed for viruses containing A21G with T23G, T23C, or T23A double mismatches in the reverse primer, with a measurable Ct value shift between the initial H3 and H3_v2 assay. The assay showed 100% agreement with expected results in 139 clinical specimens, and no cross-reactivity with other influenza subtypes or other respiratory pathogens. These findings indicate the updated H3_v2 assay improves sensitivity for contemporary and emerging seasonal A(H3N2) viruses while maintaining high specificity. This update ensures continued reliable detection of seasonal A(H3N2), including genetically drifted A(H3N2) viruses, such as the recently emerged A(H3N2) subclade K viruses, and supports global influenza surveillance and diagnostic preparedness. IMPORTANCE Accurate detection and subtyping of influenza A(H3N2) viruses are essential for clinical diagnostics, outbreak response, and global influenza surveillance. The emergence and fixation of mutations leading to primer mismatches in the FDA-cleared CDC A(H3) real-time RT-PCR assay raised concerns about reduced sensitivity for contemporary circulating A(H3N2) viruses. To address this, we redesigned the reverse primer and optimized additional assay components to improve detection of currently circulating seasonal A(H3N2) viruses. The updated H3_v2 assay restores and enhances sensitivity, including for viruses harboring key reverse primer mismatches, while maintaining high specificity and complete agreement with clinical specimens. This update ensures reliable detection of seasonal influenza A(H3N2) and A(H3N2) variant viruses, including recently emerged A(H3N2) subclade K viruses, and strengthens diagnostic preparedness and surveillance capacity nationally and internationally.

Question and background
The study aimed to address concerns about reduced sensitivity of the CDC influenza A(H3) subtyping assay for contemporary circulating influenza A(H3N2) viruses due to emerging mutations in the H3 reverse primer region.
Design and population
The study involved updating the H3 assay by redesigning the reverse primer and optimizing additional assay components to improve detection of currently circulating seasonal A(H3N2) viruses. Evaluations were conducted using cultured influenza viruses, synthetic RNA, and panels of non-influenza respiratory pathogens.
Core methods
Analytical sensitivity, inclusivity, and exclusivity were evaluated using cultured influenza viruses, synthetic RNA, and panels of non-influenza respiratory pathogens. Clinical performance was assessed using seasonal A(H3N2) residual respiratory specimens.
Main results
The updated assay (H3_v2) demonstrated analytical sensitivity comparable to or improved over the initial H3 assay, with a limit of detection of approximately 10^1.7 –10^2.1 EID50/mL and five copies of RNA per reaction. The assay showed 100% agreement with expected results in 139 clinical specimens and no cross-reactivity with other influenza subtypes or respiratory pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that the updated H3_v2 assay improves sensitivity for contemporary and emerging seasonal A(H3N2) viruses while maintaining high specificity, which is a novel enhancement to the diagnostic tool.
Scientific and public-health significance
The updated assay ensures reliable detection of seasonal influenza A(H3N2) and its variant viruses, including the recently emerged A(H3N2) subclade K viruses, thereby strengthening diagnostic preparedness and global influenza surveillance.

The study is limited to evaluating the updated assay using cultured viruses and clinical specimens, and the evidence is strong as it demonstrates 100% agreement with expected results and no cross-reactivity with other pathogens.

抗猪流感脂质纳米颗粒mRNA 疫苗的开发

Development of lipid nanoparticle mRNA vaccines against swine influenza
作者： Sophia B. Scobell, Yi-Fang Lai, Yen-Chen Chang, Hui-Wen Chen, Hui-Wen Chang, Yumian Lai, Hui‐Wen Chang
摘要中文翻译甲型猪流感病毒 (IAV-S) 可引起猪的高度传染性呼吸道疾病,并由于人与猪之间的双向 传播 造成人畜共患风险。管理猪的 IAV-S,特别是 H1 亚型,对于预防跨种 传播 和保护养猪业至关重要。脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 封装的 mRNA 疫苗 编码特定病毒抗原,提供了一个快速、适应性强的平台,非常适合针对快速进化的流感病毒株。血凝素 (HA) 是一种启动感染的病毒蛋白,由于其在流感感染中的核心作用及其抗原多样性,是 疫苗接种 的关键靶点。本研究旨在设计和生成三种编码不同 HA 抗原的 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 候选药物:台湾猪 H1、猪转人 H1 和共有 H1。然后我们在小鼠模型中评估了这些候选物的免疫原性和交叉反应潜力。体内评估表明,猪对人 H1 疫苗 候选药物在所有三种 HA 抗原上诱导了最广泛的体液 IgG 反应,而台湾猪 H1 候选药物则引发了最窄的交叉反应免疫。用所有三种候选 疫苗 免疫的小鼠的血清在不同程度上中和了台湾猪 H1N2 病毒。然而,肌内或鼻内给药均未诱导粘膜免疫。这些结果表明,虽然猪到人的 HA 构建体显示出提供广泛的系统免疫的强大潜力,但共有的 HA 构建体需要进一步优化。有效的猪流感 疫苗 策略可能需要结合肌内和鼻内方法,以确保全面的全身和粘膜免疫,包括诱导广泛中和抗体。有必要对这些候选物引起的交叉保护和细胞免疫进行进一步分析。

问题与背景
甲型猪流感病毒 (IAV-S) 可引起猪的高度传染性呼吸道疾病,并由于人与猪之间的双向 传播 造成人畜共患风险。
设计与对象
本研究旨在设计和生成三种编码不同 HA 抗原的 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 候选药物:台湾猪 H1、猪转人 H1 和共有 H1。
核心方法

Development of lipid nanoparticle mRNA vaccines against swine influenza

Authors: Sophia B. Scobell, Yi-Fang Lai, Yen-Chen Chang, Hui-Wen Chen, Hui-Wen Chang, Yumian Lai, Hui‐Wen Chang
Original abstract (English)
Swine influenza A viruses (IAV-S) cause highly contagious respiratory disease in pigs and pose zoonotic risk due to bidirectional transmission between humans and swine. Managing IAV-S, particularly the H1 subtype, in pigs is vital both to prevent interspecies transmission and to protect the swine industry. Lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-encapsulated mRNA vaccines encoding specific viral antigens offer a rapid, adaptable platform well-suited for targeting rapidly evolving influenza strains. Hemagglutinin (HA), the viral protein that initiates infection, is a critical target for vaccination due to its central role in influenza infection and its antigenic diversity. This study aimed to design and generate three mRNA-LNP vaccine candidates encoding distinct HA antigens: a Taiwan porcine H1, a swine-to-human H1, and a consensus H1. We then evaluated the immunogenicity and cross-reactive potential of these candidates in a mouse model. In vivo evaluation demonstrated that the swine-to-human H1 vaccine candidate induced the broadest humoral IgG response across all three HA antigens, whereas the Taiwan porcine H1 candidate elicited the narrowest cross-reactive immunity. Serum derived from mice-immunized with all three candidate vaccines neutralized the Taiwan porcine H1N2 virus to varying degrees. However, neither intramuscular nor intranasal administration induced mucosal immunity. These results suggest while the swine-to-human HA construct shows strong potential for providing broad systemic immunity, the consensus HA construct requires further optimization. An effective swine influenza vaccine strategy may require a combined intramuscular and intranasal approach to ensure comprehensive systemic and mucosal immunity, including the induction of broadly neutralizing antibodies. Further analysis of the cross-protection and cellular immunity elicited by these candidates is necessary.

计算设计的多亚型铭文 疫苗 具有针对 H1 和 H3 猪甲型流感病毒的广谱保护

Computationally designed multisubtype epigraph vaccine confers broad-spectrum protection against H1 and H3 swine influenza A viruses
作者： Adthakorn Madapong, Joshua Wiggins, Eric A. Weaver
摘要中文翻译H1 和 H3 亚型甲型猪流感病毒 (IAV-S) 威胁全球农业经济并造成人畜共患风险,但传统的 疫苗 针对其巨大的抗原多样性提供的保护有限。在这里,我们利用表达计算设计的 H1 和 H3 印记血凝素 (HA) 免疫原的腺病毒 媒介 开发了多亚型 疫苗。在小鼠中,与市售的 疫苗 相比,这种多合一配方可引发平衡的体液和细胞免疫,并表现出针对多种 IAV-S 分离株的中和广度。多亚型递送系统同时引发针对 H1 和 H3 亚型的免疫和保护。多合一 疫苗 赋予针对异源 H1 和 H3 挑战的保护作用,并且不会加剧不匹配的 疫苗接种 的临床发病特征。这些发现证明了计算优化和多重 疫苗 在不影响安全性的情况下提供针对不断变化的流感病毒的广谱保护的可行性。

Computationally designed multisubtype epigraph vaccine confers broad-spectrum protection against H1 and H3 swine influenza A viruses

Authors: Adthakorn Madapong, Joshua Wiggins, Eric A. Weaver
Original abstract (English)
H1 and H3 subtype swine influenza A viruses (IAV-S) threaten global agricultural economies and pose zoonotic risks, yet conventional vaccines provide limited protection against their vast antigenic diversity. Here, we developed a multisubtype vaccine utilizing adenoviral vectors expressing computationally designed H1 and H3 epigraph hemagglutinin (HA) immunogens. In mice, this All-in-One formulation elicited balanced humoral and cellular immunity and demonstrated neutralizing breadth against diverse IAV-S isolates compared to a commercially available vaccine. The multisubtype delivery system elicited concurrent immunity and protection against both H1 and H3 subtypes. The All-in-One vaccine conferred protection against heterologous H1 and H3 challenges and did not exacerbate clinical morbidity characteristic of mismatched vaccination. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of computationally optimized and multiplexed vaccines providing broad-spectrum protection against evolving influenza viruses without compromising safety.

Question and background
H1 and H3 subtype swine influenza A viruses (IAV-S) threaten global agricultural economies and pose zoonotic risks, yet conventional vaccines provide limited protection against their vast antigenic diversity.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
In mice, this All-in-One formulation elicited balanced humoral and cellular immunity and demonstrated neutralizing breadth against diverse IAV-S isolates compared to a commercially available vaccine.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings demonstrate the feasibility of computationally optimized and multiplexed vaccines providing broad-spectrum protection against evolving influenza viruses without compromising safety.
Scientific and public-health significance
The All-in-One vaccine conferred protection against heterologous H1 and H3 challenges and did not exacerbate clinical morbidity characteristic of mismatched vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The multisubtype delivery system elicited concurrent immunity and protection against both H1 and H3 subtypes.

甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染后针对甲型流感 (H5) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒的交叉保护

Cross-Protection against Influenza A(H5) Clade 2.3.4.4b Viruses after A(H1N1)pdm09 Infection
作者： Xiang-Jie Sun, Jessica A. Belser, Zhu-Nan Li, Nicole Brock, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza, Troy J. Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Jessie C. Chang, Paul J. Carney
摘要中文翻译结果显示,之前的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒免疫对这两种病毒具有部分保护作用,与 H5N1 相比,对 H5N5 的保护作用略有减弱,这可能反映了不存在交叉反应性 N5 神经氨酸酶抗体。我们使用免疫前雪貂模型来比较先前 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染对具有同源或异源 NA 亚型的 A(H5) 病毒的交叉保护。经疾病控制和预防中心动物护理和使用委员会批准,我们在生物安全 3 级增强设施中进行了所有 H5N5 和 H5N1 病毒实验。与我们的研究结果一致,尽管可检测到 N1 反应,但人类中的 N5 交叉反应性有限 (10)。我们的研究结果表明,先前的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒感染在雪貂中赋予了针对 H5N1 和 H5N5 病毒的部分交叉保护,从而降低了疾病的严重程度、致死率和病毒传播。免责声明:早期发布的文章不被视为最终版本。任何更改将在文章正式发布当月反映在在线版本中。第 32 卷第 12 期 - 2026 年 12 月 研究信件 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染后针对甲型流感 (H5) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒的交叉保护 本文建议引用 摘要 我们用 H5N1 或 H5N5 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒对先前感染流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒的雪貂进行挑战,以评估交叉保护。甲型流感 (H5) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒已在野生鸟类和家禽中广泛传播,并引起包括人类在内的哺乳动物感染,引发了公共卫生问题 (1)。2025年,美国华盛顿报告了一例人类感染H5N5分支2.3.4.4b病毒的病例;患者随后死亡,代表...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估先前感染 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒是否可以提供针对 H5N1 和 H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒的交叉保护,这些病毒由于在鸟类和哺乳动物中传播而引起了公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
该研究使用了免疫前雪貂模型,其中 12 只雪貂在血清学上对 季节性流感 病毒不敏感,并用 H5N1 和 H5N5 病毒对它们进行攻击。每组三只雪貂在感染后第 4 天被安乐死,以评估病毒传播情况。
核心方法
研究人员用 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒感染雪貂,并使用血凝抑制 (HAI) 测定证实了血清转化。然后,他们用 H5N1 和 H5N5 病毒攻击这些未免疫的雪貂,并监测临床症状、体重减轻和病毒脱落。
主要结果
结果显示,先前的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 免疫可对 H5N1 和 H5N5 病毒提供部分保护,但对 H5N5 病毒的保护略有减弱,这可能是由于缺乏交叉反应性 N5 神经氨酸酶抗体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染的免疫力可能对 H5N1 和 H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒提供一些交叉保护,突出了 HA 茎抗体在这种交叉反应中的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于了解针对新出现的 H5N1 和 H5N5 病毒的交叉保护具有重要意义,这可以为 监测 和 疫苗 的开发策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于雪貂模型,并未考虑人类 免疫应答,但由于受控的实验设计和明确的结果,证据是强有力的。

Cross-Protection against Influenza A(H5) Clade 2.3.4.4b Viruses after A(H1N1)pdm09 Infection

Authors: Xiang-Jie Sun, Jessica A. Belser, Zhu-Nan Li, Nicole Brock, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza, Troy J. Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Jessie C. Chang, Paul J. Carney
Original abstract (English)
Results show that previous A(H1N1)pdm09 virus immunity conferred partial protection against both viruses, with slightly reduced protection against H5N5 compared with H5N1, potentially reflecting the absence of cross-reactive N5 neuraminidase antibodies. We used a preimmune ferret model to compare cross-protection conferred by previous A(H1N1)pdm09 infection against A(H5) viruses with homologous or heterologous NA subtypes. We conducted all experiments with H5N5 and H5N1 viruses in a Biosafety Level 3 enhanced facility with approval from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee. Consistent with our findings, limited N5 cross-reactivity has been reported in humans despite detectable N1 responses (10). Our findings showed that previous A(H1N1)pdm09 viral infection conferred partial cross-protection against H5N1 and H5N5 viruses in ferrets, reducing disease severity, lethality, and viral dissemination. Disclaimer: Early release articles are not considered as final versions. Any changes will be reflected in the online version in the month the article is officially released. Volume 32, Number 12—December 2026 Research Letter Cross-Protection against Influenza A(H5) Clade 2.3.4.4b Viruses after A(H1N1)pdm09 Infection Suggested citation for this article Abstract We challenged ferrets previously infected with influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 virus with H5N1 or H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses to assess cross-protection. Influenza A(H5) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have spread widely among wild birds and poultry and caused infections in mammals, including humans, raising public health concerns (1). A case of human infection with H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b virus was reported in Washington, USA during 2025; the patient subsequently died, representing the seco...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether prior infection with A(H1N1)pdm09 virus provides cross-protection against H5N1 and H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, which have raised public health concerns due to their spread among birds and mammals.
Design and population
The study used a preimmune ferret model, with 12 ferrets serologically naive to seasonal influenza viruses, and challenged them with H5N1 and H5N5 viruses. Three ferrets per group were euthanized on day 4 postinfection to assess viral dissemination.
Core methods
The researchers infected ferrets with A(H1N1)pdm09 virus and confirmed seroconversion using a hemagglutination inhibition (HAI) assay. They then challenged these preimmune ferrets with H5N1 and H5N5 viruses and monitored clinical signs, weight loss, and viral shedding.
Main results
Results showed that prior A(H1N1)pdm09 immunity conferred partial protection against both H5N1 and H5N5 viruses, with slightly reduced protection against H5N5, potentially due to the absence of cross-reactive N5 neuraminidase antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that immunity from A(H1N1)pdm09 infection may offer some cross-protection against H5N1 and H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, highlighting the potential role of HA stalk antibodies in this cross-reactivity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have implications for understanding cross-protection against emerging H5N1 and H5N5 viruses, which could inform surveillance and vaccine development strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a ferret model and does not account for human immune responses, but the evidence is strong due to the controlled experimental design and clear results.

用于 3D 气道球体和流感感染建模的氧化纤维素纳米纤维水凝胶

Oxidized Cellulose Nanofiber Hydrogels for 3D Airway Spheroids and Influenza Infection Modeling
作者： Jingyi Tang, Boyu Zheng, Mathias Dimde, Katharina Achazi, Chuanxiong Nie, Nan Ma, Rainer Haag
摘要中文翻译摘要:迫切需要能够概括组织结构和免疫能力的微生理系统(MPS)来模拟呼吸道病毒感染并加速 抗病毒药物 的发现。在这里,我们创建了一种基于气道球体的 MPS,由氧化纤维素纳米纤维 (OCNF) 水凝胶支持,它提供了水合的细胞外基质 (ECM) 模拟纳米纤维微环境,并支持稳定的长期 3D 培养。气道上皮样球体可以单独由 Calu-3 细胞生成,也可以作为包含极化 THP-1 巨噬细胞样细胞的免疫活性共培养球体生成,以捕获上皮免疫串扰。OCNF 平台可在 6 天内促进球体快速自组装,并保持结构完整性 ≥ 20 天,从而允许对甲型流感病毒复制和 宿主 固有 免疫应答 进行纵向询问。值得注意的是,该模型揭示人骨髓间充质干细胞 (MSC) 发挥有效的 抗病毒药物 活性,通过激活干扰素 (IFN) 相关途径(包括 MX1、OAS1 和 STAT1)显着减少流感感染。总之,这种支持 OCNF 水凝胶的气道球体 MPS 为 抗病毒药物 干预措施的机械研究和临床前评估提供了可扩展且可重复的测试平台,并建立了一个针对个性化患者相关呼吸道感染模型的体外平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种微生理系统(MPS),该系统可以模拟呼吸道病毒感染,并通过概括组织结构和免疫能力来支持 抗病毒药物 的发现。
设计与对象
该研究使用氧化纤维素纳米纤维 (OCNF) 水凝胶创建了一种基于气道球体的 MPS,它支持稳定的长期 3D 培养,并允许对甲型流感病毒复制和 宿主 免疫应答 进行纵向分析。
核心方法
气道上皮样球体由 Calu-3 细胞产生,或作为与极化 THP-1 巨噬细胞样细胞的免疫活性共培养物产生。OCNF 水凝胶平台能够在 6 天内快速球体自组装,并保持结构完整性 ≥ 20 天。
主要结果
OCNF水凝胶平台允许气道球体在6天内快速自组装,并保持其结构完整性至少20天,促进甲型流感病毒复制和宿主 免疫应答的纵向研究。
解释与创新
该模型证明,人骨髓来源的间充质干细胞 (MSC) 对甲型流感病毒具有有效的 抗病毒药物 活性,可通过激活 IFN 相关途径显着减少感染。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果建立了一个用于 抗病毒药物 干预措施临床前评估的体外平台,并为与 季节性流感 相关的个性化呼吸道感染模型提供了可扩展的方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外模型,不包括体内验证,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于缺乏临床数据,证据是中等的。

Oxidized Cellulose Nanofiber Hydrogels for 3D Airway Spheroids and Influenza Infection Modeling

Authors: Jingyi Tang, Boyu Zheng, Mathias Dimde, Katharina Achazi, Chuanxiong Nie, Nan Ma, Rainer Haag
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Micro‐physiological systems (MPS) that recapitulate tissue architecture and immune competence are urgently needed to model respiratory viral infections and accelerate antiviral discovery. Here, we create an airway spheroid‐based MPS supported by an oxidized cellulose nanofiber (OCNF) hydrogel, which provides a hydrated, extracellular matrix (ECM) mimetic nanofibrillar microenvironment and supports stable long‐term 3D culture. Airway epithelial‐like spheroids are generated either from Calu‐3 cells alone or as immunocompetent co‐culture spheroids incorporating polarized THP‐1 macrophage‐like cells to capture epithelial‐immune crosstalk. The OCNF platform promotes rapid spheroid self‐assembly within 6 days and preserves structural integrity for ≥ 20 days, allowing longitudinal interrogation of influenza A virus replication and host innate immune responses. Notably, the model reveals that human bone marrow‐derived mesenchymal stem cells (MSCs) exert potent antiviral activity, significantly reducing influenza infection through activation of interferon (IFN)‐associated pathways, including MX1, OAS1, and STAT1. Together, this OCNF hydrogel‐enabled airway spheroid MPS provides a scalable and reproducible test‐bed for mechanistic studies and preclinical evaluation of antiviral interventions and establishes an in vitro platform toward personalized patient‐relevant respiratory infection models.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a micro-physiological system (MPS) that can model respiratory viral infections and support antiviral discovery by recapitulating tissue architecture and immune competence.
Design and population
The study created an airway spheroid-based MPS using oxidized cellulose nanofiber (OCNF) hydrogel, which supports stable long-term 3D culture and allows for longitudinal analysis of influenza A virus replication and host immune responses.
Core methods
Airway epithelial-like spheroids were generated from Calu-3 cells or as immunocompetent co-cultures with polarized THP-1 macrophage-like cells. The OCNF hydrogel platform enabled rapid spheroid self-assembly within 6 days and preserved structural integrity for ≥ 20 days.
Main results
The OCNF hydrogel platform allowed for the rapid self-assembly of airway spheroids within 6 days and maintained their structural integrity for at least 20 days, facilitating longitudinal study of influenza A virus replication and host immune responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The model demonstrated that human bone marrow-derived mesenchymal stem cells (MSCs) have potent antiviral activity against influenza A virus, significantly reducing infection through activation of IFN-associated pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings establish an in vitro platform for preclinical evaluation of antiviral interventions and offer a scalable method for personalized respiratory infection models relevant to seasonal influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro models and does not include in vivo validation, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate due to the absence of clinical data.

咖啡酸乙酯可恢复 BNIP3L 依赖性线粒体自噬和线粒体功能,从而改善甲型流感病毒引起的肺损伤

Ethyl caffeate restores BNIP3L-dependent mitophagy and mitochondrial function to ameliorate influenza A virus-induced lung injury
作者： 黄志纯, Lara Teixeira Lopes, Jiaming Long, Lu Wang, Haojun Liang, Kunyuan Li, Xiaohong Chen, 谌建新, Xin Zhao, Kexin Wang
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染会引起严重的肺损伤,其特征是过度炎症和线粒体功能障碍,其中线粒体清除功能受损起着至关重要的作用。咖啡酸乙酯 (EC) 是一种源自膳食的生物活性酚类化合物,具有有效的抗炎和抗氧化特性。然而,其对 IAV 引发的肺损伤和线粒体质量控制功能障碍的保护作用仍不清楚。本研究在小鼠和细胞中建立了PR8诱导的IAV感染模型,以评估EC的保护作用。EC 治疗 改善了生存结果,以独立于直接病毒抑制的方式显着减轻了肺损伤,减少了促炎细胞因子的表达,并改善了线粒体功能。从机制上讲,EC 挽救了 IAV 感染引起的受损自噬流,p62 积累减少和线粒体清除增强证明了这一点,而这种作用需要完整的溶酶体降解活性。蛋白质组学分析进一步确定 BNIP3L 是一个关键的调节因子,并且 BNIP3L 的基因敲除很大程度上消除了 EC 介导的保护表型。EC 显着上调 BNIP3L 表达,促进线粒体清除并维持线粒体稳态。代谢组学分析表明,EC 显着重塑了与肺线粒体功能相关的 宿主 代谢谱,葫芦巴碱被确定为 EC 干预后的关键代谢物。功能实验表明,葫芦巴碱通过促进自噬体-溶酶体融合来恢复 BNIP3L 依赖性线粒体清除并减弱体外和体内炎症反应,从而重现 EC 样保护作用。总体而言,EC 通过恢复 BNIP3L 介导的线粒体清除和线粒体稳态来减轻 IAV 引起的肺损伤。葫芦巴碱作为这一保护过程的功能代谢物和代谢特征,突显了植物源性酚类化合物、线粒体清除调节和病毒感染期间 宿主 代谢重编程之间的潜在联系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染会引起严重的肺损伤,其特征是过度炎症和线粒体功能障碍,其中线粒体清除功能受损起着至关重要的作用。
设计与对象
本研究在小鼠和细胞中建立了PR8诱导的IAV感染模型,以评估EC的保护作用。
核心方法
EC 治疗 用于评估其对生存结果、肺损伤、促炎细胞因子表达和线粒体功能的影响。从机制上讲,该研究检查了自噬通量、溶酶体降解活性和蛋白质组分析,以确定 BNIP3L 作为关键调节因子。
主要结果
EC 治疗 改善了生存结果,以独立于直接病毒抑制的方式显着减轻了肺损伤,减少了促炎细胞因子的表达,并改善了线粒体功能。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,EC 通过恢复 BNIP3L 介导的线粒体清除和线粒体稳态来减轻 IAV 引起的肺损伤。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果凸显了 EC 作为减轻 IAV 感染引起的肺损伤的治疗剂的潜力,为病毒感染期间线粒体质量控制提供了新的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内模型,证据基于机械和功能实验,可能无法完全转化为人类临床环境。

Ethyl caffeate restores BNIP3L-dependent mitophagy and mitochondrial function to ameliorate influenza A virus-induced lung injury

Authors: 黄志纯, Lara Teixeira Lopes, Jiaming Long, Lu Wang, Haojun Liang, Kunyuan Li, Xiaohong Chen, 谌建新, Xin Zhao, Kexin Wang
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) infection induces severe lung injury characterized by excessive inflammation and mitochondrial dysfunction, in which impaired mitochondrial clearance plays a crucial role. Ethyl caffeate (EC), a bioactive phenolic compound of dietary origin, exhibits potent anti-inflammatory and antioxidant properties. However, its protective role against IAV-triggered lung damage and mitochondrial quality control dysfunction remains poorly defined. In this study, PR8-induced IAV infection models was established in mice and cells to evaluate the protective effects of EC. EC treatment improved survival outcomes, markedly alleviated lung injury in a manner independent of direct viral inhibition, reduced pro-inflammatory cytokine expression, and improved mitochondrial function. Mechanistically, EC rescued the impaired autophagy flux induced by IAV infection, as evidenced by reduced p62 accumulation and enhanced mitochondrial clearance, and this effect required intact lysosomal degradation activity. Proteomic analysis further identified BNIP3L as a key regulator, and genetic knockdown of BNIP3L largely abolished EC-mediated protective phenotypes. EC significantly upregulated BNIP3L expression to promote mitochondrial clearance and maintain mitochondrial homeostasis. Metabolomic analysis showed that EC markedly reshaped host metabolic profiles associated with mitochondrial function in lung, with trigonelline identified as a key metabolite upon EC intervention. Functional experiments demonstrated that trigonelline recapitulated EC-like protective effects by facilitating autophagosome-lysosome fusion to restore BNIP3L-dependent mitochondrial clearance and attenuating inflammatory responses both in vitro and in vivo . Overall, EC alleviates IAV-induced lung injury by restoring BNIP3L-mediated mitochondrial clearance and mitochondrial homeostasis. Trigonelline serves as a functional metabolite and metabolic signature of this protective process, highlighting a potential link between plant-derived phenolic compounds, mitochondrial clearance regulation, and host metabolic reprogramming during viral infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus (IAV) infection induces severe lung injury characterized by excessive inflammation and mitochondrial dysfunction, in which impaired mitochondrial clearance plays a crucial role.
Design and population
In this study, PR8-induced IAV infection models were established in mice and cells to evaluate the protective effects of EC.
Core methods
EC treatment was used to assess its impact on survival outcomes, lung injury, pro-inflammatory cytokine expression, and mitochondrial function. Mechanistically, the study examined autophagy flux, lysosomal degradation activity, and proteomic analysis to identify BNIP3L as a key regulator.
Main results
EC treatment improved survival outcomes, markedly alleviated lung injury in a manner independent of direct viral inhibition, reduced pro-inflammatory cytokine expression, and improved mitochondrial function.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that EC alleviates IAV-induced lung injury by restoring BNIP3L-mediated mitochondrial clearance and mitochondrial homeostasis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the potential of EC as a therapeutic agent for mitigating lung injury caused by IAV infection, offering new insights into mitochondrial quality control during viral infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo models, and the evidence is based on mechanistic and functional experiments, which may not fully translate to human clinical settings.

疫苗接种前镇痛药对流感 疫苗 免疫原性和有效性的影响。

The Effect of Prevaccination Analgesics on Influenza Vaccine Immunogenicity and Effectiveness.
作者： Carlie N Skellington, Kat Schmidt, Christina Schofield, Anuradha Ganesan, Tahaniyat Lalani, Katrin Mende, Adam Saperstein, Alan Williams, Derek Larson, Bruce McClenathan
摘要中文翻译背景:有时在 疫苗接种 之前服用镇痛药物,以防止局部和全身不良反应。尽管研究已经检验了疫苗接种后镇痛剂使用与免疫原性之间的关联,但很少有人评估疫苗接种前镇痛剂使用的效果。该分析评估了流感 疫苗接种 之前使用或未使用镇痛剂的成年人的抗体反应、流感样疾病 (ILI) 和流感感染。方法:DoD 流感 疫苗 有效性实用评估 (PAIVED) 研究是一项开放标签、随机试验,评估 FDA 许可的流感 疫苗 在 4 个流感季节对成年军事医疗受益人的有效性。在 疫苗接种 之前 24 小时内拥有镇痛使用数据的 PAIVED 参与者,如果他们提供了疫苗接种前和疫苗接种后静脉血样本或参加了每周 ILI 监测,则纳入本分析。结果:在 684 名接种前和接种后抗体滴度的 PAIVED 参与者中,10.4% (n = 71) 报告在流感 疫苗接种 之前使用过镇痛剂。多变量分析显示镇痛剂的使用与几何平均滴度倍数变化之间没有关联。与不使用镇痛剂相比,使用对乙酰氨基酚与 A/H3N2 流感血清转化增加之间存在适度关联(aIRR = 1.37;P = .022);然而,这种关联在调整多重比较的敏感性分析中并不显着。在拥有 ILI 监测 数据的 10 723 名 PAIVED 参与者中,10.6% (n = 1140) 报告接种前使用镇痛剂。多变量分析显示镇痛药的使用与 ILI 的 发病率 或流感感染的几率之间没有关联。结论:这些研究结果表明,在流感 疫苗接种 发生前 24 小时内使用对乙酰氨基酚和/或非甾体抗炎镇痛药不会降低 疫苗 成人的免疫原性或有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
有时在 疫苗接种 之前服用镇痛药物,以防止局部和全身不良反应。尽管研究已经检验了疫苗接种后镇痛剂使用与免疫原性之间的关联,但很少有人评估疫苗接种前镇痛剂使用的效果。
设计与对象
国防部流感 疫苗 有效性的实用评估 (PAIVED) 研究是一项开放标签、随机试验,评估 FDA 许可的流感 疫苗 在 4 个流感季节对成年军事医疗保健受益人的有效性。在 疫苗接种 之前 24 小时内拥有镇痛使用数据的 PAIVED 参与者,如果他们提供了疫苗接种前和疫苗接种后静脉血样本或参加了每周 ILI 监测,则纳入本分析。
核心方法
该分析评估了流感 疫苗接种 之前使用或未使用镇痛剂的成年人的抗体反应、流感样疾病 (ILI) 和流感感染。
主要结果
在 684 名接种前和接种后抗体滴度的 PAIVED 参与者中,10.4% (n = 71) 报告在流感 疫苗接种 之前使用了镇痛剂。多变量分析显示镇痛剂的使用与几何平均滴度倍数变化之间没有关联。与不使用镇痛剂相比,使用对乙酰氨基酚与 A/H3N2 流感血清转化增加之间存在适度关联(aIRR = 1.37;P = .022);然而,这种关联在调整多重比较的敏感性分析中并不显着。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,在流感 疫苗接种 发生前 24 小时内使用对乙酰氨基酚和/或非甾体抗炎镇痛药不会降低 疫苗 成人的免疫原性或有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,疫苗接种前使用镇痛药不会损害成人流感 疫苗 的免疫原性或有效性,从而为公共卫生实践提供谨慎的信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于自我报告的镇痛药物使用情况以及可能存在的混杂因素。由于分析的观察性质和敏感性调整的需要,证据的强度中等。

The Effect of Prevaccination Analgesics on Influenza Vaccine Immunogenicity and Effectiveness.

Authors: Carlie N Skellington, Kat Schmidt, Christina Schofield, Anuradha Ganesan, Tahaniyat Lalani, Katrin Mende, Adam Saperstein, Alan Williams, Derek Larson, Bruce McClenathan
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Analgesic medications are sometimes taken prior to vaccination to prevent local and systemic adverse reactions. Although studies have examined the association between postvaccination analgesic use and immunogenicity, few have evaluated the effects of prevaccination analgesic use. This analysis assessed antibody response, influenza-like illness (ILI), and influenza infection among adults with and without analgesic use before influenza vaccination. METHODS: The Pragmatic Assessment of Influenza Vaccine Effectiveness in the DoD (PAIVED) study was an open-label, randomized trial evaluating the effectiveness of FDA-licensed influenza vaccines during 4 influenza seasons in adult military healthcare beneficiaries. PAIVED participants with data on analgesic use within 24 hours before vaccination were included in this analysis if they either provided prevaccination and postvaccination venous blood samples or participated in weekly ILI surveillance. RESULTS: Among 684 PAIVED participants with prevaccination and postvaccination antibody titers, 10.4% (n = 71) reported analgesic use before influenza vaccination. Multivariable analyses showed no association between analgesic use and geometric mean titer fold change. There was a modest association between acetaminophen use and increased seroconversion to influenza A/H3N2 compared with no analgesic use (aIRR = 1.37; P = .022); however, this association was not significant in sensitivity analyses adjusting for multiple comparisons. Among 10 723 PAIVED participants with ILI surveillance data, 10.6% (n = 1140) reported prevaccination analgesic use. Multivariable analyses revealed no association between analgesic use and incidence of ILI or odds of influenza infection. CONCLUSIONS: These findings suggest acetaminophen and/or nonsteroidal anti-inflammatory analgesic use within 24 hours before influenza vaccination does not reduce vaccine immunogenicity or effectiveness in adults.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Analgesic medications are sometimes taken prior to vaccination to prevent local and systemic adverse reactions. Although studies have examined the association between postvaccination analgesic use and immunogenicity, few have evaluated the effects of prevaccination analgesic use.
Design and population
The Pragmatic Assessment of Influenza Vaccine Effectiveness in the DoD (PAIVED) study was an open-label, randomized trial evaluating the effectiveness of FDA-licensed influenza vaccines during 4 influenza seasons in adult military healthcare beneficiaries. PAIVED participants with data on analgesic use within 24 hours before vaccination were included in this analysis if they either provided prevaccination and postvaccination venous blood samples or participated in weekly ILI surveillance.
Core methods
This analysis assessed antibody response, influenza-like illness (ILI), and influenza infection among adults with and without analgesic use before influenza vaccination.
Main results
Among 684 PAIVED participants with prevaccination and postvaccination antibody titers, 10.4% (n = 71) reported analgesic use before influenza vaccination. Multivariable analyses showed no association between analgesic use and geometric mean titer fold change. There was a modest association between acetaminophen use and increased seroconversion to influenza A/H3N2 compared with no analgesic use (aIRR = 1.37; P = .022); however, this association was not significant in sensitivity analyses adjusting for multiple comparisons.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest acetaminophen and/or nonsteroidal anti-inflammatory analgesic use within 24 hours before influenza vaccination does not reduce vaccine immunogenicity or effectiveness in adults.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can cautiously inform public health practices by suggesting that prevaccination analgesic use does not compromise the immunogenicity or effectiveness of influenza vaccines in adults.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its reliance on self-reported analgesic use and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the analysis and the need for sensitivity adjustments.

基于三年聚合酶链反应的 监测 成人呼吸道感染和季节性模式。

Three-Year Polymerase Chain Reaction-Based Surveillance of Respiratory Tract Infections in Adults and Seasonal Patterns.
作者： Ling Xue, Yuhui Ye, Yuanxiang Li
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查2023-2025年江西省赣州市成人呼吸道感染的病原学构成、季节变化及危险因素。
设计与对象
2023年至2025年,在江西省赣州市一家三级医院开展了一项为期三年的基于聚合酶链反应的监测研究,纳入了8,493名有呼吸道症状的成年人(≥18岁)。
核心方法
使用多重实时聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测口咽和鼻咽拭子,针对六类七种呼吸道病原体,包括甲型和乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)、副流感病毒I型和III型、腺病毒和肺炎支原体。通过单变量分析和多变量逻辑回归分析数据。
主要结果
The overall pathogen detection rate was 4.14% (352/8,493), comprising 325 single infections (92.33%) and 27 mixed infections (7.67%). Among single infections, Mycoplasma pneumoniae (39.69%), influenza A virus (28.00%) and RSV (16.00%) predominated. Infections peaked in winter–spring (December–March). Age ≥65 years (OR = 2.35, 95% CI: 1.78–3.09), underlying diseases (OR = 2.41, 95% CI: 1.89–3.08) and emergency department admission (OR = 1.58, 95% CI: 1.24–2.01) were independent risk factors.
解释与创新
作者解读,赣州成人呼吸道感染呈现明显的冬春季节性,以肺炎支原体、甲型流感病毒和RSV为主要病原。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持针对年龄≥65岁的成年人和患有基础疾病的人的季节性目标监测和优先疫苗接种、扩大的诊断组合和基于早期PCR的诊断。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖基于 PCR 的检测,且重点关注单一三级医院,这可能会影响普适性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Three-Year Polymerase Chain Reaction-Based Surveillance of Respiratory Tract Infections in Adults and Seasonal Patterns.

Authors: Ling Xue, Yuhui Ye, Yuanxiang Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objective: This study investigated the aetiological composition, seasonal variation and risk factors of adult respiratory tract infections in Ganzhou, Jiangxi Province, during 2023–2025. Methods: A total of 8,493 adults (≥18 years) with respiratory symptoms were enrolled from a tertiary hospital during 2023–2025. Oropharyngeal and nasopharyngeal swabs were tested using multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction (PCR) panels targeting six categories of seven respiratory pathogens: influenza A and B viruses, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), parainfluenza virus types I and III, adenovirus and Mycoplasma pneumoniae. Mixed infections (two or more pathogens) were reported separately. Data were analysed by univariate analysis and multivariate logistic regression. Results: The overall pathogen detection rate was 4.14% (352/8,493), comprising 325 single infections (92.33%) and 27 mixed infections (7.67%). Among single infections, M. pneumoniae (39.69%), influenza A virus (28.00%) and RSV (16.00%) predominated. Infections peaked in winter–spring (December–March). Age ≥65 years (OR = 2.35, 95% CI: 1.78–3.09), underlying diseases (OR = 2.41, 95% CI: 1.89–3.08) and emergency department admission (OR = 1.58, 95% CI: 1.24–2.01) were independent risk factors. Conclusion: Adult respiratory infections in Ganzhou show clear winter–spring seasonality, with M. pneumoniae, influenza A virus and RSV as leading pathogens. These findings support seasonally targeted surveillance – with enhanced monitoring for M. pneumoniae and influenza during winter–spring months – and prioritised vaccination, expanded diagnostic panels and early PCR-based diagnosis for adults aged ≥65 years and those with underlying diseases, thereby strengthening local respiratory infection prevention and control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the aetiological composition, seasonal variation and risk factors of adult respiratory tract infections in Ganzhou, Jiangxi Province, during 2023–2025.
Design and population
A three-year polymerase chain reaction-based surveillance study was conducted at a tertiary hospital in Ganzhou, Jiangxi Province, from 2023 to 2025, enrolling 8,493 adults (≥18 years) with respiratory symptoms.
Core methods
Oropharyngeal and nasopharyngeal swabs were tested using multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction (PCR) panels targeting six categories of seven respiratory pathogens, including influenza A and B viruses, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), parainfluenza virus types I and III, adenovirus and Mycoplasma pneumoniae. Data were analysed by univariate analysis and multivariate logistic regression.
Main results
The overall pathogen detection rate was 4.14% (352/8,493), comprising 325 single infections (92.33%) and 27 mixed infections (7.67%). Among single infections, Mycoplasma pneumoniae (39.69%), influenza A virus (28.00%) and RSV (16.00%) predominated. Infections peaked in winter–spring (December–March). Age ≥65 years (OR = 2.35, 95% CI: 1.78–3.09), underlying diseases (OR = 2.41, 95% CI: 1.89–3.08) and emergency department admission (OR = 1.58, 95% CI: 1.24–2.01) were independent risk factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that adult respiratory infections in Ganzhou show clear winter–spring seasonality, with Mycoplasma pneumoniae, influenza A virus and RSV as leading pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support seasonally targeted surveillance and prioritised vaccination, expanded diagnostic panels and early PCR-based diagnosis for adults aged ≥65 years and those with underlying diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on PCR-based detection and its focus on a single tertiary hospital, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.
