伊朗对流感 疫苗 的个人偏好:离散选择实验。

Individual Preferences for Influenza Vaccine in Iran: A Discrete Choice Experiment.
作者： Sanaz Parvizi, Mohsen Mehrara, Ali Taiebnia
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景和目的 流感 疫苗接种 是针对 季节性流感 的关键预防措施。了解公众对流感 疫苗 属性的偏好对于设计有效的 疫苗接种 策略至关重要。本研究调查了伊朗个人对流感 疫苗 属性的偏好,并确定了与 疫苗 选择相关的因素。方法 2024 年 9 月至 12 月期间,在德黑兰省的 226 名成年人中进行了离散选择实验 (DCE)。评估了 疫苗 的七个属性:有效性、严重副作用风险、轻度副作用风险、剂量次数、保护持续时间、制造地点和价格。使用条件和混合 Logit 模型来估计参与者的偏好,并使用对数似然、Akaike 信息准则 (AIC) 和贝叶斯信息准则 (BIC) 评估模型性能。结果 有效性较高的 疫苗 更有可能被选择,而严重副作用风险较高和价格较高则与选择 疫苗 的可能性较低相关。尽管混合 Logit 模型表明对国产 疫苗 的偏好较低,但在条件 Logit 模型中,轻度副作用的风险、保护持续时间和生产地点与 疫苗 选择的关联有限或没有关联。观察到偏好异质性,年龄、婚姻状况、教育和健康保险状况与特定 疫苗 属性的偏好差异相关。结论 疫苗 的有效性、安全性和可负担性似乎是本研究中与流感 疫苗 选择相关的最重要属性。这些研究结果表明,强调 疫苗 有效性和安全性同时提高负担能力的公共卫生策略可能有助于增加伊朗对流感 疫苗 的吸收。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感疫苗接种是针对季节性流感的关键预防措施。了解公众对流感 疫苗 属性的偏好对于设计有效的 疫苗接种 策略至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究调查了伊朗个人对流感 疫苗 属性的偏好,并确定了与 疫苗 选择相关的因素。2024 年 9 月至 12 月期间,在德黑兰省的 226 名成年人中进行了一项离散选择实验 (DCE)。
核心方法
评估了 疫苗 的七个属性:有效性、严重副作用的风险、轻微副作用的风险、剂量数量、保护持续时间、制造地点和价格。使用条件和混合 Logit 模型来估计参与者的偏好,并使用对数似然、Akaike 信息准则 (AIC) 和贝叶斯信息准则 (BIC) 评估模型性能。
主要结果
具有较高有效性的 疫苗 更有可能被选择,而严重副作用和较高价格的风险较高则与选择 疫苗 的可能性较低相关。尽管混合 Logit 模型表明对国产 疫苗 的偏好较低,但在条件 Logit 模型中,轻度副作用的风险、保护持续时间和生产地点与 疫苗 选择的关联有限或没有关联。
解释与创新
这些研究结果表明,强调 疫苗 有效性和安全性同时提高负担能力的公共卫生策略可能有助于增加伊朗对流感 疫苗 的吸收。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会强调 疫苗 有效性、安全性和可负担性在影响个人 疫苗 选择方面的重要性,从而为伊朗的公共卫生战略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
观察到偏好异质性,年龄、婚姻状况、教育和健康保险状况与特定 疫苗 属性的偏好差异相关。证据是温和的,因为该研究是基于离散选择实验,样本量有限且自我报告的偏好存在潜在偏差。

Individual Preferences for Influenza Vaccine in Iran: A Discrete Choice Experiment.

Authors: Sanaz Parvizi, Mohsen Mehrara, Ali Taiebnia
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims Influenza vaccination is a key preventive measure against seasonal influenza. Understanding public preferences for influenza vaccine attributes is essential for designing effective vaccination strategies. This study examined individuals’ preferences for influenza vaccine attributes in Iran and identified factors associated with vaccine choice. Methods A discrete choice experiment (DCE) was conducted among 226 adults in Tehran province between September and December 2024. Seven vaccine attributes were evaluated: effectiveness, risk of severe side effects, risk of mild side effects, number of doses, duration of protection, location of manufacture, and price. Conditional and mixed logit models were used to estimate participants’ preferences, and model performance was assessed using the log‐likelihood, Akaike information criterion (AIC), and Bayesian information criterion (BIC). Results Vaccines with higher effectiveness were more likely to be selected, whereas higher risks of severe side effects and higher prices were associated with a lower likelihood of vaccine selection. The risk of mild side effects, duration of protection, and location of manufacture showed limited or no association with vaccine choice in the conditional logit model, although the mixed logit model suggested a lower preference for domestically manufactured vaccines. Preference heterogeneity was observed, with age, marital status, education, and health insurance status being associated with differences in preferences for specific vaccine attributes. Conclusion Vaccine effectiveness, safety, and affordability appeared to be the most important attributes associated with influenza vaccine choice in this study. These findings suggest that public health strategies emphasizing vaccine effectiveness and safety while improving affordability may help increase influenza vaccine uptake in Iran.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza vaccination is a key preventive measure against seasonal influenza. Understanding public preferences for influenza vaccine attributes is essential for designing effective vaccination strategies.
Design and population
This study examined individuals’ preferences for influenza vaccine attributes in Iran and identified factors associated with vaccine choice. A discrete choice experiment (DCE) was conducted among 226 adults in Tehran province between September and December 2024.
Core methods
Seven vaccine attributes were evaluated: effectiveness, risk of severe side effects, risk of mild side effects, number of doses, duration of protection, location of manufacture, and price. Conditional and mixed logit models were used to estimate participants’ preferences, and model performance was assessed using the log‐likelihood, Akaike information criterion (AIC), and Bayesian information criterion (BIC).
Main results
Vaccines with higher effectiveness were more likely to be selected, whereas higher risks of severe side effects and higher prices were associated with a lower likelihood of vaccine selection. The risk of mild side effects, duration of protection, and location of manufacture showed limited or no association with vaccine choice in the conditional logit model, although the mixed logit model suggested a lower preference for domestically manufactured vaccines.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that public health strategies emphasizing vaccine effectiveness and safety while improving affordability may help increase influenza vaccine uptake in Iran.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform public health strategies in Iran by highlighting the importance of vaccine effectiveness, safety, and affordability in influencing individual vaccine choices.
Limitations and evidence strength
Preference heterogeneity was observed, with age, marital status, education, and health insurance status being associated with differences in preferences for specific vaccine attributes. The evidence is moderate as the study is based on a discrete choice experiment with a limited sample size and potential biases in self-reported preferences.

COVID-19 大流行前后气候驱动的流感亚型阳性率扰动:观察研究。

Climate-Driven Perturbations in Influenza Subtype Positivity Before and After the COVID-19 Pandemic: Observational Study.
作者： Jiajia Lin, Congzhu Li, Lirong Zhang, Jiaxiong Zheng, Yixiao Niu, Di Lin, Yan Che, Zicheng Cao, 张丽蓉
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 COVID-19 大流行显着改变了全球流感 传播 模式。大流行后,气象条件、能见度和流感亚型(A/H1N1、A/H3N2 和 B/Victoria)阳性之间的关联是否发生变化仍不清楚。我们比较了中国北方 COVID-19 大流行前后的这些关联。目的 本研究旨在描述 COVID-19 大流行前后气象变量和能见度与流感亚型阳性之间关联的变化,并确定亚型特异性的非线性模式和阈值。方法 使用 13 年的流感 监测 和环境数据,我们应用沙普利附加解释 (SHAP) 增强的极限梯度增强来表征环境变量与流感亚型阳性之间关联的相对重要性和方向。随后使用广义加性模型(GAM)来表征非线性关联模式并识别模型估计关联方向发生变化的阈值。结果绝对湿度(AH)仍然是对 A/H3N2 和 A/H1N1 影响最大的环境因素,分别占相对特征重要性的 32.2% 和 38.5%。疫情爆发后,能见度变得更加重要,从第四位上升到第二位最具影响力的环境指标。GAM 分析确定了与关联方向发生变化的点相对应的阈值。对于A/H3N2,低于4 g/m 3 的AH 与正SHAP 值相关,而较高的AH 主要与负SHAP 值相关。对于 A/H1N1,在大流行后时期,关联方向在 AH 阈值 4.5 g/m 3 (95% CI 4.0-4.8) 处发生变化。此外,相对湿度 (RH) 与 A/H3N2 阳性呈复杂的非线性关联,大流行前的转变点分别为 45.1% (95% CI 43.5%-46.1%) 和 69.7% (95% CI 69.2%-70.2%),以及 48.7% (95% CI 46.7%-50.4%) 和 70.6% (95% CI 66.3%-72.6%)在大流行后时期。这些范围内的 RH 值与负 SHAP 值相关,而这些范围之外的值与正 SHAP 值相关。相比之下,大流行后 A/H3N2 和 B/Victoria 亚型的可见性显示出相反的关联模式。能见度低于 17.1 公里 (95% CI 16.3-17.4) 与 A/H3N2 的负 SHAP 值相关,而能见度低于 18.4 公里 (95% CI 18.0-18.9) 与 B/Victoria 的正 SHAP 值相关。结论 AH 仍然是甲型流感亚型阳性的关键环境决定因素,而大流行后可见性变得更加重要,并且显示出 A/H3N2 和 B/Victoria 的相反关联模式。确定的非线性阈值突出了亚型特定的环境关联,并可能为流感 监测 和不断变化的环境条件下的风险评估提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to investigate how the associations between meteorological conditions,visibility,and influenza subtype positivity changed before and after the COVID-19 pandemic.背景强调,大流行显着改变了全球流感 传播 模式。
设计与对象
这项观察性研究利用 13 年的流感 监测 和环境数据,比较了大流行前后中国北方气象变量和能见度与流感亚型阳性的关联。
核心方法
使用 Shapley 附加解释 (SHAP) 增强的极限梯度增强方法来评估环境变量与流感亚型阳性之间关联的相对重要性和方向。然后应用广义加性模型 (GAM) 来识别非线性模式和阈值。
主要结果
绝对湿度 (AH) 仍然是 A/H3N2 和 A/H1N1 影响最大的因素,分别占相对特征重要性的 32.2% 和 38.5%。大流行之后,能见度变得更加重要,成为第二大有影响力的环境指标。对于A/H3N2,低于4 g/m 3 的AH 与正SHAP 值相关,而较高的AH 与负SHAP 值相关。对于 A/H1N1,关联方向在 AH 阈值为 4.5 g/m 3 时发生变化 (95% CI 4.0-4.8)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,绝对湿度 (AH) 仍然是甲型流感亚型阳性的关键环境决定因素,而能见度在大流行后变得更加重要,并显示出 A/H3N2 和 B/维多利亚的相反关联模式。这项研究确定了亚型特异性的非线性阈值,强调了环境因素在流感动态中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为流感 监测 和不断变化的环境条件下的风险评估提供信息,特别是了解大流行相关的变化如何影响亚型特定的环境关联。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,基于环境和 监测 数据,可能会引入偏差和混杂因素。证据是强有力的,因为它使用了先进的统计方法,并为环境协会提供了具体的阈值。

Climate-Driven Perturbations in Influenza Subtype Positivity Before and After the COVID-19 Pandemic: Observational Study.

Authors: Jiajia Lin, Congzhu Li, Lirong Zhang, Jiaxiong Zheng, Yixiao Niu, Di Lin, Yan Che, Zicheng Cao, 张丽蓉
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The COVID-19 pandemic significantly altered global influenza transmission patterns. Whether the associations among meteorological conditions, visibility, and influenza subtype (A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and B/Victoria) positivity changed after the pandemic remains unclear. We compared these associations before and after the COVID-19 pandemic in northern China. Objective This study aimed to characterize changes in the associations of meteorological variables and visibility with influenza subtype positivity before and after the COVID-19 pandemic and to identify subtype-specific nonlinear patterns and thresholds. Methods Using 13 years of influenza surveillance and environmental data, we applied Extreme Gradient Boosting enhanced with Shapley additive explanations (SHAP) to characterize the relative importance and direction of associations between environmental variables and influenza subtype positivity. Generalized additive models (GAMs) were subsequently used to characterize nonlinear association patterns and identify thresholds at which the direction of model-estimated associations changed. Results Absolute humidity (AH) remained the most influential environmental factor for A/H3N2 and A/H1N1, accounting for 32.2% and 38.5% of relative feature importance, respectively. Visibility increased in importance after the pandemic, rising from being the fourth to the second most influential environmental indicator. GAM analysis identified thresholds corresponding to points where the direction of the associations changed. For A/H3N2, AH below 4 g/m 3 was associated with positive SHAP values, whereas higher AH was predominantly associated with negative SHAP values. For A/H1N1, the direction of association changed at an AH threshold of 4.5 g/m 3 (95% CI 4.0-4.8) in the postpandemic period. Additionally, relative humidity (RH) demonstrated a complex nonlinear association with A/H3N2 positivity, with transition points at 45.1% (95% CI 43.5%-46.1%) and 69.7% (95% CI 69.2%-70.2%) in the prepandemic period and at 48.7% (95% CI 46.7%-50.4%) and 70.6% (95% CI 66.3%-72.6%) in the postpandemic period. RH values within these ranges were associated with negative SHAP values, whereas values outside these ranges were associated with positive SHAP values. In contrast, visibility showed opposing association patterns for the A/H3N2 and B/Victoria subtypes after the pandemic. Visibility below 17.1 km (95% CI 16.3-17.4) was associated with negative SHAP values for A/H3N2, whereas visibility below 18.4 km (95% CI 18.0-18.9) was associated with positive SHAP values for B/Victoria. Conclusions AH remained a key environmental determinant of influenza A subtype positivity, while visibility became more important after the pandemic and showed opposing association patterns for A/H3N2 and B/Victoria. The identified nonlinear thresholds highlight subtype-specific environmental associations and may inform influenza surveillance and risk assessment under changing environmental conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how the associations between meteorological conditions, visibility, and influenza subtype positivity changed before and after the COVID-19 pandemic. The background highlighted that the pandemic significantly altered global influenza transmission patterns.
Design and population
This observational study compared associations of meteorological variables and visibility with influenza subtype positivity in northern China before and after the pandemic, using 13 years of influenza surveillance and environmental data.
Core methods
Extreme Gradient Boosting enhanced with Shapley additive explanations (SHAP) was used to assess the relative importance and direction of associations between environmental variables and influenza subtype positivity. Generalized additive models (GAMs) were then applied to identify nonlinear patterns and thresholds.
Main results
Absolute humidity (AH) remained the most influential factor for A/H3N2 and A/H1N1, accounting for 32.2% and 38.5% of relative feature importance, respectively. Visibility increased in importance after the pandemic, becoming the second most influential environmental indicator. For A/H3N2, AH below 4 g/m 3 was associated with positive SHAP values, whereas higher AH was associated with negative SHAP values. For A/H1N1, the direction of association changed at an AH threshold of 4.5 g/m 3 (95% CI 4.0-4.8).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that absolute humidity (AH) remained a key environmental determinant of influenza A subtype positivity, while visibility became more important after the pandemic and showed opposing association patterns for A/H3N2 and B/Victoria. This study identified subtype-specific nonlinear thresholds, highlighting the importance of environmental factors in influenza dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform influenza surveillance and risk assessment under changing environmental conditions, particularly in understanding how pandemic-related changes affect subtype-specific environmental associations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and based on environmental and surveillance data, which may introduce biases and confounding factors. The evidence is strong as it uses advanced statistical methods and provides specific thresholds for environmental associations.

儿童 11 个流感季节的流行病学变异和临床严重程度。

Epidemiologic Variability and Clinical Severity Across 11 Influenza Seasons in Children.
作者： Inês Taborda, Rita Tomé, Carolina Santos Ferreira, Raquel Oliveira Inácio, Margarida Lopes, Ana Filipa Cruz, Carolina Graça da Conceição, Diana Lourenço Gonçalves, Eva M Pereira, Bárbara Oliveiros
摘要中文翻译背景:流感是呼吸系统疾病的主要原因,极大地增加了儿童的发病率和 死亡率。季节性流感 流行病受到 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 大流行的严重破坏。临床表现和疾病严重程度是否因流感病毒类型和亚型而异仍不完全清楚。本研究旨在描述 11 个季节内实验室确诊的儿科流感病例中 流行病学、临床特征、结果和医疗保健利用的时间变化,并探讨病毒类型/亚型与临床重要结果之间的关联。方法:我们对某三级儿科医院2015年7月至2026年6月期间所有实验室确诊的急性呼吸道感染儿童流感病例进行回顾性观察研究,分析病例分布及其人口学和临床特征。结果:3105 名儿童(中位年龄为 4 岁)中检测出流感,其中大多数先前健康(82.4%)。在该 队列研究 中,17.9% 的病例需要住院治疗,其中 1.1% 需要入院重症监护,22.8% 出现并发症,包括脑炎和败血症。流感的传播模式,包括高峰活动的时间和年度流行病的持续时间,随着时间的推移而变化。在 COVID-19 大流行期间连续 18 个月没有报告病例后,2021-2022 年季节出现了大幅复苏,随后经历了 2 年的非典型全年循环,然后又恢复到通常的季节模式。甲型流感占病例的 72.3%,病毒类型和亚型交替为主,没有一致的流行病学模式。在限制型/亚型分析中,甲型H1N1流感病例的住院频率较高,并有中重度并发症,且在多变量分析后这种关联仍具有统计学显着性。结论:流感与 队列研究 医院儿童的临床重要疾病和医疗保健使用相关,最常见于其他健康儿童,这凸显了流感预防策略的重要性。在 COVID-19 大流行期间和之后,流感 流行病学 发生了显着变化,强调了继续使用 监测 的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感是呼吸道疾病的主要原因,对儿童的发病率和 死亡率 有很大影响。临床表现和疾病严重程度是否因流感病毒类型和亚型而异仍不完全清楚。
设计与对象
我们在一家三级儿科医院进行了一项回顾性观察研究,包括2015年7月至2026年6月期间在急性呼吸道感染儿童中发现的所有实验室确诊的流感病例,并分析了病例的分布及其人口学和临床特征。
核心方法
在该 队列研究 中,17.9% 的病例需要住院治疗,其中 1.1% 需要入院重症监护,22.8% 出现并发症,包括脑炎和败血症。流感的传播模式,包括高峰活动的时间和年度流行病的持续时间,随着时间的推移而变化。在限制型/亚型分析中,甲型H1N1流感病例的住院频率较高,并有中重度并发症,且在多变量分析后这种关联仍具有统计学显着性。
主要结果
3105 名儿童(中位年龄为 4 岁)中检测出流感,其中大多数先前健康(82.4%)。甲型流感占病例的 72.3%,病毒类型和亚型交替为主,没有一致的流行病学模式。
解释与创新
在这家医院的 队列研究 中,流感与儿童的临床重要疾病和医疗保健使用相关,最常见于其他健康的儿童,这凸显了流感预防策略的重要性。在 COVID-19 大流行期间和之后,流感 流行病学 发生了显着变化,强调了继续使用 监测 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了流感预防策略的重要性,并强调在 COVID-19 大流行期间和之后继续 监测 的必要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Epidemiologic Variability and Clinical Severity Across 11 Influenza Seasons in Children.

Authors: Inês Taborda, Rita Tomé, Carolina Santos Ferreira, Raquel Oliveira Inácio, Margarida Lopes, Ana Filipa Cruz, Carolina Graça da Conceição, Diana Lourenço Gonçalves, Eva M Pereira, Bárbara Oliveiros
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Influenza is a major cause of respiratory illness, contributing substantially to morbidity and mortality in children. Seasonal influenza epidemics were profoundly disrupted by the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic. Whether the clinical presentation and disease severity differ according to influenza virus type and subtype remains incompletely understood. This study aimed to describe temporal changes in epidemiology, clinical characteristics, outcomes and healthcare utilization among laboratory-confirmed pediatric influenza cases over 11 seasons and to explore associations between virus type/subtype and clinically important outcomes. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective observational study at a tertiary pediatric hospital, including all laboratory-confirmed influenza cases identified in children presenting with acute respiratory infection between July 2015 and June 2026, and analyzed the distribution of cases and their demographic and clinical characteristics. RESULTS: Influenza was detected in 3105 children (median age, 4 years), the majority of whom were previously healthy (82.4%). Within this cohort, hospitalization was required in 17.9% of cases, with 1.1% requiring intensive care admission, and 22.8% developing complications, including encephalitis and sepsis. Influenza circulation patterns, including the timing of peak activity and the duration of annual epidemics, varied over time. After an 18-month absence of reported cases during the COVID-19 pandemic, the 2021-2022 season was marked by a substantial resurgence, followed by 2 years of atypical year-round circulation before returning to the usual seasonal pattern. Influenza A accounted for 72.3% of cases, with alternating predominance of virus types and subtypes without a consistent epidemiologic pattern. In a restricted type/subtype analysis, influenza A(H1N1) cases had a higher frequency of hospitalization and recorded moderate-to-severe complications, and this association remained statistically significant after multivariable analysis. CONCLUSIONS: Influenza was associated with clinically important illness and healthcare use among children in this hospital-based cohort, most commonly among otherwise healthy children, highlighting the importance of influenza prevention strategies. During and after the COVID-19 pandemic, influenza epidemiology changed markedly, underscoring the need for continued surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza is a major cause of respiratory illness, contributing substantially to morbidity and mortality in children. Whether the clinical presentation and disease severity differ according to influenza virus type and subtype remains incompletely understood.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective observational study at a tertiary pediatric hospital, including all laboratory-confirmed influenza cases identified in children presenting with acute respiratory infection between July 2015 and June 2026, and analyzed the distribution of cases and their demographic and clinical characteristics.
Core methods
Within this cohort, hospitalization was required in 17.9% of cases, with 1.1% requiring intensive care admission, and 22.8% developing complications, including encephalitis and sepsis. Influenza circulation patterns, including the timing of peak activity and the duration of annual epidemics, varied over time. In a restricted type/subtype analysis, influenza A(H1N1) cases had a higher frequency of hospitalization and recorded moderate-to-severe complications, and this association remained statistically significant after multivariable analysis.
Main results
Influenza was detected in 3105 children (median age, 4 years), the majority of whom were previously healthy (82.4%). Influenza A accounted for 72.3% of cases, with alternating predominance of virus types and subtypes without a consistent epidemiologic pattern.
Interpretation and novelty
Influenza was associated with clinically important illness and healthcare use among children in this hospital-based cohort, most commonly among otherwise healthy children, highlighting the importance of influenza prevention strategies. During and after the COVID-19 pandemic, influenza epidemiology changed markedly, underscoring the need for continued surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of influenza prevention strategies and underscore the need for continued surveillance during and after the COVID-19 pandemic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

流感 疫苗接种 和法国因流感住院的老年人的疾病严重程度:一项全国范围的 队列研究 研究,2016-2024 年

Influenza Vaccination and Disease Severity Among Older Adults Hospitalized with Influenza in France: A Nationwide Cohort Study, 2016-2024
作者： Claire Nour Abou Chakra, Aby Ba Diallo, Andre Some, Baptiste Pitel, Hélène Bricout, Clotilde El Guerche‐Séblain, Cécile Chauvel, Marta Coelho Nunes, Clotilde EL GUERCHE-SEBLAIN
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 疫苗 可用,但 季节性流感 仍然是老年人严重发病和 死亡率 的主要原因。我们评估了法国 65 岁或以上因流感住院的成年人中,流感 疫苗接种 是否与疾病严重程度降低相关。
设计与对象
使用法国国家健康数据系统 (SNDS) 进行了一项全国性回顾性 队列研究 研究。包括 2016 年 10 月至 2024 年 12 月期间因确诊流感(ICD-10 代码:J09 或 J10)住院的 65 岁或以上成年人,不包括 2020 年至 2021 年。
核心方法
疫苗接种 状态是从报销的社区药房配药记录中确定的。加权回归模型估计了有创机械通气 (IMV)、重症监护病房 (ICU) 入住、院内死亡和复合严重结局的调整风险比 (aRR)。
主要结果
Among 132,443 influenza-associated hospitalizations, 72,852 (55%) occurred among vaccinated patients and 59,591 (45%) among unvaccinated patients. IMV occurred in 2128 vaccinated (2.9%) and 2544 unvaccinated (4.3%) hospitalizations (aRR, 0.88; 95%CI: 0.83 to 0.93). In-hospital death occurred in 6300 (8.6%) and 4952 (8.3%), respectively (aRR, 0.90; 95%CI: 0.87 to 0.93). Vaccination was also associated with a lower risk of the composite outcome (aRR, 0.96; 95%CI: 0.94 to 0.98), but not ICU admission (aRR, 0.99; 95%CI: 0.96 to 1.01).
解释与创新
在这项全国性的 队列研究 研究中,流感 队列研究 与因流感住院的老年人中较低的 IMV 和院内死亡风险相关。这些发现与疾病严重程度可能减弱的情况一致,并支持改善老年人流感 疫苗接种 的努力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持改善老年人流感 疫苗接种 的努力,这可能会降低该弱势群体的疾病严重程度和 死亡率。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于全国范围的回顾性 队列研究 设计,可能会引入偏差和混杂因素。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的未测量的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Influenza Vaccination and Disease Severity Among Older Adults Hospitalized with Influenza in France: A Nationwide Cohort Study, 2016-2024

Authors: Claire Nour Abou Chakra, Aby Ba Diallo, Andre Some, Baptiste Pitel, Hélène Bricout, Clotilde El Guerche‐Séblain, Cécile Chauvel, Marta Coelho Nunes, Clotilde EL GUERCHE-SEBLAIN
Original abstract (English)
Objective: Seasonal influenza remains a major cause of severe morbidity and mortality among older adults, despite vaccine availability. We assessed whether influenza vaccination was associated with reduced disease severity among adults aged 65 years or more hospitalized with influenza in France. Methods and Analysis We conducted a nationwide retrospective cohort study using the French National Health Data System (SNDS). Adults aged 65 years or more hospitalized with confirmed influenza (ICD-10 codes: J09 or J10) between October 2016 and December 2024 were included, excluding 2020-2021. Vaccination status was identified from reimbursed community pharmacy dispensing records. Entropy balancing was used to balance sociodemographic characteristics, chronic conditions pneumococcal vaccination, and COVID-19 vaccination. Weighted regression models estimated adjusted risk ratios (aRRs) for invasive mechanical ventilation (IMV), intensive care unit (ICU) admission, in-hospital death, and a composite severe outcome. Multistate survival models assessed transitions specific hazard ratios (HR) between hospitalization, ICU, discharge, and in-hospital death. Results Among 132,443 influenza-associated hospitalizations, 72,852 (55%) occurred among vaccinated patients and 59,591 (45%) among unvaccinated patients. Vaccinated patients were older (mean age, 82.3 vs 79.3 years). IMV occurred in 2128 vaccinated (2.9%) and 2544 unvaccinated (4.3%) hospitalizations (aRR, 0.88; 95%CI: 0.83 to 0.93). In-hospital death occurred in 6300 (8.6%) and 4952 (8.3%), respectively (aRR, 0.90; 95%CI: 0.87 to 0.93). Vaccination was also associated with a lower risk of the composite outcome (aRR, 0.96; 95%CI: 0.94 to 0.98), but not ICU admission (aRR, 0.99; 95%CI: 0.96 to 1.01). Among patients admitted to ICU, vaccination was associated with a lower hazard of death (adjusted hazard ratio, 0.85; 95%CI: 0.78 to 0.92) and a 2-day reduction in the median time to discharge. Conclusion In this nationwide cohort study, influenza vaccination was associated with lower risks of IMV and in-hospital death among older adults hospitalized with influenza. These findings are consistent with possible attenuation of disease severity and support efforts to improve influenza vaccination among older adults.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza remains a major cause of severe morbidity and mortality among older adults, despite vaccine availability. We assessed whether influenza vaccination was associated with reduced disease severity among adults aged 65 years or more hospitalized with influenza in France.
Design and population
A nationwide retrospective cohort study was conducted using the French National Health Data System (SNDS). Adults aged 65 years or more hospitalized with confirmed influenza (ICD-10 codes: J09 or J10) between October 2016 and December 2024 were included, excluding 2020-2021.
Core methods
Vaccination status was identified from reimbursed community pharmacy dispensing records. Weighted regression models estimated adjusted risk ratios (aRRs) for invasive mechanical ventilation (IMV), intensive care unit (ICU) admission, in-hospital death, and a composite severe outcome.
Main results
Among 132,443 influenza-associated hospitalizations, 72,852 (55%) occurred among vaccinated patients and 59,591 (45%) among unvaccinated patients. IMV occurred in 2128 vaccinated (2.9%) and 2544 unvaccinated (4.3%) hospitalizations (aRR, 0.88; 95%CI: 0.83 to 0.93). In-hospital death occurred in 6300 (8.6%) and 4952 (8.3%), respectively (aRR, 0.90; 95%CI: 0.87 to 0.93). Vaccination was also associated with a lower risk of the composite outcome (aRR, 0.96; 95%CI: 0.94 to 0.98), but not ICU admission (aRR, 0.99; 95%CI: 0.96 to 1.01).
Interpretation and novelty
In this nationwide cohort study, influenza vaccination was associated with lower risks of IMV and in-hospital death among older adults hospitalized with influenza. These findings are consistent with possible attenuation of disease severity and support efforts to improve influenza vaccination among older adults.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support efforts to improve influenza vaccination among older adults, which could lead to reduced disease severity and mortality in this vulnerable population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a nationwide retrospective cohort design, which may introduce biases and confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and potential unmeasured confounders.

以准确度、精度和覆盖范围重建系统发育的替代历史。

Reconstructing substitution histories on phylogenies, with accuracy, precision, and coverage.
作者： Jordan Douglas, Lindell D. Bromham
摘要中文翻译祖先序列和替代历史通常在系统发育推断中进行平均,因此不会报告给用户。然而,它们可以通过祖先序列重建(ASR)和随机字符映射来恢复。通过实施和验证与单位点和多物种聚结分析兼容的新 ASR 和随机作图包,我们展示了重建取代历史如何增强获得的有关分子 进化 的节奏和模式的信息。我们观察到,与缺乏时间方向性(最大似然和贝叶斯)的无约束和非超度量替代树相比,已知的替代历史(模拟和经验)在时间树上恢复得更准确和精确。我们表明,基于四个核苷酸字符的具有位点速率异质性的密码子划分模型可以有效地近似同义和非同义变化的历史,同时比需要 61 个字符的计算要求高的密码子模型所需的运行时间要少得多。反过来,这提供了 dN/dS 的低偏差估计,对 GC 含量、密码子使用偏差和核苷酸交换率的非平稳性具有鲁棒性。最后,通过三个案例研究,我们证明了该方法如何恢复分子 进化 和病原体 传播 的预期模式:i) 体型较小的哺乳动物物种往往比体型较大的亲属具有更快的同义替换率,ii) 氨基酸替换与氨酰基-tRNA 合成酶中的 3Di 结构变化相关,以及 iii) 在主要的 H3N2 病毒中,甲型流感病毒传播到附近的位置比远处的位置更频繁新西兰的 暴发。当基于随机映射得出的计数估计(相对于参数率和距离)时,这三种趋势更加明显。我们的开源代码带有用户友好的图形界面,如BEAST 2的BeastMap包;在贝叶斯系统发育框架内支持各种时钟、位点和树模型和数据类型(包括插入和删除)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过提高重建替代历史的准确性和精确度来解决系统发育推断的局限性,替代历史通常会被平均而不报告给用户。
设计与对象
该研究实施并验证了新的祖先序列重建(ASR)和随机映射包,通过涉及 季节性流感 病毒的三个案例研究展示了其应用。
核心方法
这些方法涉及开发一个与单基因座和多物种合并分析兼容的软件包,使用贝叶斯系统发育框架来支持各种时钟、位点和树模型,包括插入和删除。
主要结果
与无约束和非超度量替代树相比,已知的替代历史(模拟的和经验的)在时间树上恢复得更加准确和精确。
解释与创新
作者解释说,他们的方法提供了更可靠的 dN/dS 估计,这对 GC 含量、密码子使用偏差和核苷酸交换率的非平稳性具有鲁棒性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以帮助理解 季节性流感 病毒中的分子 进化 模式,特别是与病毒蛋白的 传播 动力学和结构变化有关。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认,基于四个核苷酸特征的具有位点速率异质性的密码子分配模型可以比计算要求较高的模型更有效地近似替代历史,尽管证据是基于模拟和经验数据。

Reconstructing substitution histories on phylogenies, with accuracy, precision, and coverage.

Authors: Jordan Douglas, Lindell D. Bromham
Original abstract (English)
Ancestral sequences and substitution histories are usually averaged out in phylogenetic inference and are therefore not reported to the user. However, they can be recovered through ancestral sequence reconstruction (ASR) and stochastic character mapping. By implementing and validating a new ASR and stochastic mapping package compatible with both single-locus and multispecies coalescent analysis, we show how reconstructing substitution histories can enhance the information gained about the tempo and mode of molecular evolution. We observe that known substitution histories (simulated and empirical) are recovered more accurately and precisely on time trees compared with unconstrained and non-ultrametric substitution trees that lack temporal directionality (maximum likelihood and Bayesian). We show that codon-partition models with site-rate heterogeneity, based on the four nucleotide characters, can effectively approximate the histories of synonymous and non-synonymous change, while requiring far less runtime than computationally demanding codon models that require 61 characters. In turn, this provides low-bias estimation of dN/dS that is robust to non-stationarity in GC content, codon usage bias, and nucleotide exchangeability rates. Lastly, using three case studies, we demonstrate how the approach can recover expected patterns in molecular evolution and pathogen transmission: that i) smaller-bodied mammalian species tend to have faster synonymous substitution rates than larger relatives, ii) amino acid substitutions are correlated with 3Di structural changes in aminoacyl-tRNA synthetases, and iii) influenza A virus spread more frequently to nearby locations than distant ones, in a major H3N2 outbreak in New Zealand. These three trends were more pronounced when based on count estimates derived from stochastic mapping, relative to parametric rates and distances. Our open-source code comes with a user-friendly graphical interface, as the BeastMap package for BEAST 2; supporting a wide range of clock, site, and tree models and data types - including insertions and deletions - within a Bayesian phylogenetic framework.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of phylogenetic inference by improving the accuracy and precision of reconstructing substitution histories, which are typically averaged out and not reported to users.
Design and population
The study implemented and validated a new ancestral sequence reconstruction (ASR) and stochastic mapping package, demonstrating its application through three case studies involving seasonal influenza viruses.
Core methods
The methods involved developing a package compatible with both single-locus and multispecies coalescent analysis, using a Bayesian phylogenetic framework to support various clock, site, and tree models, including insertions and deletions.
Main results
Known substitution histories, both simulated and empirical, were recovered more accurately and precisely on time trees compared to unconstrained and non-ultrametric substitution trees.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that their approach provides a more reliable estimation of dN/dS, which is robust to non-stationarity in GC content, codon usage bias, and nucleotide exchangeability rates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the understanding of molecular evolution patterns in seasonal influenza viruses, particularly in relation to transmission dynamics and structural changes in viral proteins.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that codon-partition models with site-rate heterogeneity based on four nucleotide characters can approximate substitution histories more efficiently than computationally demanding models, though the evidence is based on simulated and empirical data.

COVID-19 大流行后老年流感住院患者的结局:功能独立性下降和虚弱。

Outcomes of elderly hospitalized patients with influenza after the COVID-19 pandemic: decline of functional independence and frailty.
作者： Tadashi Ishida, Akihiko Ito, Yosuke Nakanishi, Kazuhiro Tateda, Jiro Fujita, Koichi Izumikawa, Hideaki Nagai, Masafumi Seki, 彰彦 伊藤
摘要中文翻译背景:流感仍然是老年人的沉重负担,并且情况受到了 COVID-19 大流行的影响。本研究的目的是澄清 COVID-19 大流行后患有流感的老年住院患者的结局。方法:收集 2023 年 9 月至 2026 年 3 月(COVID-19 大流行后的 3 个流感季节)期间,65 岁或以上患有严重流感并需要在一个中心住院的患者的临床数据。对它们的结果、功能独立性和脆弱性进行了分析。结果:共有210名老年患者入组。中位年龄79岁,入院前根据临床衰弱量表(CFS)判断为轻度以上衰弱者32例。肺炎是导致住院的最常见疾病,其次是全身状况不佳和心力衰竭。中位住院时间为 13 天,17 名患者 (8.1%) 发生院内死亡。60例因进一步治疗或日常生活能力恶化而转院,37例(17.6%)的功能独立测量(FIM)即使在康复后仍低于70分。19 例(9.0%)出院时 CFS 恶化。结论:在 COVID-19 大流行之后,流感感染仍然是老年人的沉重负担。老年人往往会失去功能独立性,即使在急性疾病康复后也不会变得虚弱。避免入院的流感预防措施(包括 疫苗接种)至关重要。(流感互联网调查,UMIN000015989)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感仍然是老年人的沉重负担,并且情况受到了 COVID-19 大流行的影响。
设计与对象
本研究的目的是澄清 COVID-19 大流行后患有流感的老年住院患者的结局。2023 年 9 月至 2026 年 3 月(COVID-19 大流行后的 3 个流感季节)收集了 65 岁或以上患有严重流感并需要在一个中心住院的患者的临床数据。
核心方法
对它们的结果、功能独立性和脆弱性进行了分析。
主要结果
共有210名老年患者入组。中位年龄79岁,入院前根据临床衰弱量表(CFS)判断为轻度以上衰弱者32例。肺炎是导致住院的最常见疾病,其次是全身状况不佳和心力衰竭。中位住院时间为 13 天,17 名患者 (8.1%) 发生院内死亡。60例因进一步治疗或日常生活能力恶化而转院,37例(17.6%)的功能独立测量(FIM)即使在康复后仍低于70分。19 例(9.0%)出院时 CFS 恶化。
解释与创新
COVID-19 大流行后,流感感染仍然是老年人的沉重负担。老年人往往会失去功能独立性,即使在急性疾病康复后也不会变得虚弱。
科研与公卫意义
避免入院的流感预防措施(包括 疫苗接种)至关重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Outcomes of elderly hospitalized patients with influenza after the COVID-19 pandemic: decline of functional independence and frailty.

Authors: Tadashi Ishida, Akihiko Ito, Yosuke Nakanishi, Kazuhiro Tateda, Jiro Fujita, Koichi Izumikawa, Hideaki Nagai, Masafumi Seki, 彰彦 伊藤
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Influenza remains a heavy burden to the elderly and the situation has been affected by the COVID-19 pandemic. The objective of this study was to clarify outcomes of elderly hospitalized patients with influenza after the COVID-19 pandemic. METHODS: The clinical data of patients 65 years of age or older who were severely ill with influenza and required hospitalization in one center were gathered between September 2023 and March 2026 (3 influenza seasons after the COVID-19 pandemic). The outcomes, functional independence, and frailty of them were analyzed. RESULTS: A total of 210 elderly patients were enrolled. The median age was 79 and 32 cases were judged as having mild or more frailty by the clinical frailty scale (CFS) before admission. Pneumonia was the most common disease indicated as cause for hospitalization, followed by a poor general condition and heart failure. The median duration of hospitalization was 13 days and in-hospital deaths were recorded for 17 patients (8.1%). Sixty cases changed hospital due to further treatment or deterioration of activity of daily living, and the functional independence measure (FIM) of 37 cases (17.6%) remained under a score of 70 even after rehabilitation. The CFSs worsened in 19 cases (9.0%) at the time of discharge. CONCLUSIONS: Influenza infection remains a heavy burden among the elderly after the COVID-19 pandemic. The elderly tend to lose functional independence and fail to frailty even after recovery from acute illness. Preventive measures for influenza to avoid admission, including vaccination, will be essential. (Influenza Internet Survey, UMIN000015989).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza remains a heavy burden to the elderly and the situation has been affected by the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
The objective of this study was to clarify outcomes of elderly hospitalized patients with influenza after the COVID-19 pandemic. The clinical data of patients 65 years of age or older who were severely ill with influenza and required hospitalization in one center were gathered between September 2023 and March 2026 (3 influenza seasons after the COVID-19 pandemic).

The outcomes, functional independence, and frailty of them were analyzed.
Main results
A total of 210 elderly patients were enrolled. The median age was 79 and 32 cases were judged as having mild or more frailty by the clinical frailty scale (CFS) before admission. Pneumonia was the most common disease indicated as cause for hospitalization, followed by a poor general condition and heart failure. The median duration of hospitalization was 13 days and in-hospital deaths were recorded for 17 patients (8.1%). Sixty cases changed hospital due to further treatment or deterioration of activity of daily living, and the functional independence measure (FIM) of 37 cases (17.6%) remained under a score of 70 even after rehabilitation. The CFSs worsened in 19 cases (9.0%) at the time of discharge.
Interpretation and novelty
Influenza infection remains a heavy burden among the elderly after the COVID-19 pandemic. The elderly tend to lose functional independence and fail to frailty even after recovery from acute illness.
Scientific and public-health significance
Preventive measures for influenza to avoid admission, including vaccination, will be essential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study.

使用不同口罩进行超声洁牙期间气溶胶和飞溅传播的比较:与呼吸道感染相关的初步研究 传播

Comparison of Dissemination of Aerosol and Splatter during Ultrasonic Scaling using Different Masks: A Pilot Study with Relevance to Respiratory Infection Transmission
作者： Samidha Vivek Jambhekar
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:牙医经常暴露在大量的微生物负荷下,因为口腔是众所周知的巨大细菌负荷的来源。此外,由于COVID-19最近影响了全世界,因此口罩被认为是抑制传播的综合措施策略。然而,气溶胶和呼吸道感染控制的相关性不仅限于COVID-19,因为包括季节性流感病毒(例如流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09,通常称为猪流感)、流感甲型H3N2流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)和其他呼吸道病毒在内的呼吸道病原体继续循环,并可能通过呼吸道颗粒传播。目的:比较使用不同面罩进行超声洁治时气溶胶和飞溅的传播情况。方法:在本研究中,我们通过预先标记的一厘米见方的刻度尺计算滤纸的表面积,评估了四种口罩的传播情况,即N-95带呼吸器、N-95不带呼吸器、布口罩和N-95不带呼吸器的组合以及普通外科口罩。结果:结合使用双重口罩(无过滤器的 N-95 加布口罩)可提供最佳保护,其次是单独的 N-95 口罩,最后是外科口罩。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决牙科环境中有效感染控制措施的需求,因为牙医经常接触口腔中的大量微生物,这是一种已知的呼吸道病原体 储存宿主。背景强调了口罩在预防 传播 呼吸道感染(包括 季节性流感 病毒)方面的重要性。
设计与对象
该试点研究评估了使用不同面罩进行超声洁牙期间气溶胶和飞溅的传播情况。测试的口罩包括带呼吸器的N-95、不带呼吸器的N-95、布口罩和不带呼吸器的N-95的组合以及普通外科口罩。
核心方法
该研究使用预先标记的一厘米见方的刻度来测量滤纸的表面积,以评估不同口罩在超声波缩放过程中抑制气溶胶和飞溅的有效性。
主要结果
无过滤器的N-95加布口罩的组合使用提供了最好的保护,其次是单独的N-95口罩,然后是外科口罩。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,N-95 和布口罩的双重口罩可在牙科手术过程中提供卓越的保护,防止气溶胶传播,这与预防 传播 呼吸道病原体(例如 季节性流感 病毒)有关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果对于改善牙科环境中的感染控制策略具有重要意义,特别是对于减少 季节性流感 病毒等呼吸道病原体通过气溶胶和飞溅的传播。
局限与证据强度
作为一项试点研究,结果可能无法推广到所有临床环境,并且证据仅限于超声波洁牙和测试的面罩类型的特定背景。该研究对其研究结果提供了适度的信心。

Comparison of Dissemination of Aerosol and Splatter during Ultrasonic Scaling using Different Masks: A Pilot Study with Relevance to Respiratory Infection Transmission

Authors: Samidha Vivek Jambhekar
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Dentists are routinely exposed to a high amount of micro-organism load due to primarily being responsible for the treatment of the structures if the mouth – which is a well-known source of huge bacterial loads. Also, as COVID-19 recently affected the entire world, masks thus were considered to act as a comprehensive strategy of measures to suppress transmission. However, the relevance of aerosol and respiratory infection control extends beyond COVID-19, as respiratory pathogens including seasonal influenza viruses such as influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, commonly referred to as swine flu, influenza A(H3N2), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and other respiratory viruses continue to circulate and may be transmitted through respiratory particles. Aim: To compare the dissemination of aerosol and splatter during ultrasonic scaling using different masks. Method: In this study we evaluated the dissemination of four masks that is N-95 with respirator, N-95 without respirator, a combination of cloth mask and N-95 without respirator, and normal surgical mask by calculating the surface are of filter paper with the help of a previously marked scale which was divided into one-centimeter squares. Results: Best protection was seen to be offered by the combined use of the double masking – N-95 without filter plus cloth mask, followed by N-95 mask alone and then the surgical mask.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for effective infection control measures in dental settings, where dentists are routinely exposed to high microbial loads from the oral cavity, a known reservoir for respiratory pathogens. The background highlighted the importance of masks in preventing transmission of respiratory infections, including seasonal influenza viruses.
Design and population
This pilot study evaluated the dissemination of aerosol and splatter during ultrasonic scaling using different masks. The masks tested included N-95 with respirator, N-95 without respirator, a combination of cloth mask and N-95 without respirator, and normal surgical mask.
Core methods
The study measured the surface area of filter paper using a previously marked scale divided into one-centimeter squares to assess the effectiveness of different masks in containing aerosol and splatter during ultrasonic scaling.
Main results
The combined use of N-95 without filter plus cloth mask provided the best protection, followed by N-95 mask alone, and then the surgical mask.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that double masking with N-95 and cloth mask offers superior protection against aerosol dissemination during dental procedures, which is relevant for preventing transmission of respiratory pathogens such as seasonal influenza viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results have implications for improving infection control strategies in dental environments, particularly for reducing the spread of respiratory pathogens like seasonal influenza viruses through aerosol and splatter.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a pilot study, the results may not be generalizable to all clinical settings, and the evidence is limited to the specific context of ultrasonic scaling and mask types tested. The study provides moderate confidence in its findings.

按前季节感染划分的流感发病率和 疫苗 有效性和 疫苗接种:17 季分析。

Influenza Attack Rates and Vaccine Effectiveness by Prior-Season Infection and Vaccination: A 17-Season Analysis.
作者： Naoki Kawai, Takuma Bando, Takashi Kawashima, Shinro Matsuura, Tetsunari Maeda, Woon Joo Lee, Shin Nagao, Hareaki Yamamoto, Kazuo Mori, Tokunao Amemiya
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查前季节流感感染和 疫苗接种 如何影响当前季节的发病率和 疫苗 对 季节性流感 病毒的有效性 (VE)。疫苗 有效性通常被解释为一种保护措施,但可能会受到不同人群基线风险差异的影响。
设计与对象
在日本(2002-2003 年至 2018-2019 年)开展了一项以社区为基础的 17 个季节的前瞻性 队列研究 研究,分析了 148,108 人的季节。在由上一季节感染和 疫苗接种 定义的组之间比较当前季节未接种疫苗的个体和接种疫苗的个体的发病率。
核心方法
通过快速抗原检测确认流感感染。使用多变量逻辑回归估计当前季节 疫苗接种 的调整优势比 (aOR) 和相对 aOR,并以实验室确诊的甲型流感感染(是/否)作为结果。
主要结果
在本赛季未接种疫苗的个体中,接种前一赛季 疫苗接种 的人群的发病率 (AR) 高于未接种疫苗的人群(儿童为 14.0% vs 8.6%;P < .001)。所有组均保持保护作用(aOR < 1)。
解释与创新
研究发现,前季节感染与当前季节 疫苗接种 的 VE 增强相关,而前季节 疫苗接种 与减弱但有效的 VE 相关。这些发现强调了解释 AR 和 VE 的重要性,因为 AR 反映了基线风险的差异。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,前一季节的感染可能会提高当前季节 疫苗接种 的有效性,这对 疫苗 策略和了解流感预防的基线风险差异具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个地理区域,并依赖于自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态。证据是中等的,因为研究结果基于观察数据并针对潜在的混杂因素进行了调整。

Influenza Attack Rates and Vaccine Effectiveness by Prior-Season Infection and Vaccination: A 17-Season Analysis.

Authors: Naoki Kawai, Takuma Bando, Takashi Kawashima, Shinro Matsuura, Tetsunari Maeda, Woon Joo Lee, Shin Nagao, Hareaki Yamamoto, Kazuo Mori, Tokunao Amemiya
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Vaccine effectiveness (VE) is commonly interpreted as a measure of protection but may be influenced by differences in baseline risk across populations. The effects of prior-season influenza infection and vaccination on both attack rates (ARs) and VE have not been fully characterized. METHODS: We analyzed 148 108 person-seasons from a 17-season prospective, community-based cohort study in Japan (2002-2003 through 2018-2019). Influenza infection was confirmed using rapid antigen testing. Attack rates among current-season unvaccinated individuals (a proxy for baseline risk) and vaccinated individuals were compared across groups defined by prior-season infection and vaccination. Adjusted odds ratios (aORs) and relative aORs for current-season vaccination were estimated using multivariable logistic regression, with laboratory-confirmed influenza A infection (yes/no) as the outcome. RESULTS: Among current-season unvaccinated individuals, ARs were higher in those with prior-season vaccination than in those without (14.0% vs 8.6% in children; P < .001). Attack rates were similar regardless of prior-season infection but higher with prior-season vaccination. Current-season vaccination reduced odds of influenza A infection (aOR, 0.52; 95% CI, .47-.57). This reduction was more pronounced among individuals with prior-season infection (aOR, 0.35; 95% CI, .25-.49) and less pronounced among those with prior-season vaccination (aOR, 0.59; 95% CI, .50-.70). Protection remained across all groups (aOR < 1). CONCLUSIONS: Prior-season infection was associated with enhanced VE of current-season vaccination and similar ARs among vaccinated individuals, whereas prior-season vaccination was associated with attenuated but effective VE. These findings highlight the importance of interpreting AR alongside VE, as ARs reflect differences in baseline risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how prior-season influenza infection and vaccination affect current-season attack rates and vaccine effectiveness (VE) for seasonal influenza viruses. Vaccine effectiveness is commonly interpreted as a measure of protection but may be influenced by differences in baseline risk across populations.
Design and population
A 17-season prospective, community-based cohort study in Japan (2002-2003 through 2018-2019) was conducted, analyzing 148,108 person-seasons. Attack rates among current-season unvaccinated individuals and vaccinated individuals were compared across groups defined by prior-season infection and vaccination.
Core methods
Influenza infection was confirmed using rapid antigen testing. Adjusted odds ratios (aORs) and relative aORs for current-season vaccination were estimated using multivariable logistic regression, with laboratory-confirmed influenza A infection (yes/no) as the outcome.
Main results
Among current-season unvaccinated individuals, attack rates (ARs) were higher in those with prior-season vaccination than in those without (14.0% vs 8.6% in children; P < .001). Protection remained across all groups (aOR < 1).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that prior-season infection was associated with enhanced VE of current-season vaccination, while prior-season vaccination was associated with attenuated but effective VE. These findings highlight the importance of interpreting AR alongside VE, as ARs reflect differences in baseline risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that prior-season infection may improve the effectiveness of current-season vaccination, which has implications for vaccine strategies and understanding baseline risk differences in influenza prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single geographic region and relies on self-reported vaccination status. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on observational data and adjusted for potential confounders.

靶向 TMPRSS2 通过抑制 PAR-2/MMP-9 轴来预防流感相关肺损伤

Targeting TMPRSS2 protects against influenza-associated lung injury by suppressing PAR-2/MMP-9 axis
作者： Yin Wu, Ming Yu, Li Feng, Qifeng Peng, Juan Yang, Linsong Hou, Hong Pan, Yi Zhang, Wu Y, Yu M
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒引起的严重急性肺损伤(ALI)以肺泡通透性过高为特征,其中基质金属蛋白酶-9(MMP-9)与细胞外基质(ECM)降解和上皮屏障受损密切相关。然而,涉及病毒相关 MMP-9 上调的 宿主 调节机制仍不完全清楚。在这项研究中,我们研究了 宿主 蛋白酶 TMPRSS2 及其下游伙伴蛋白酶激活受体 2 (PAR-2) 在 A/WSN/33(H1N1) 感染期间调节 MMP-9 表达的潜在参与。我们的研究结果表明,病毒感染伴随着 TMPRSS2 介导的 PAR-2 激活,以及 MEK/ERK 和 PI3K/AKT 信号通路的下游参与以及增强的 NF-κB 活性,与升高的 MMP-9 水平相关。用卡莫司他对 TMPRSS2 进行药理学抑制可抑制 PAR-2 激活,在体外和体内减弱下游激酶磷酸化,并与 MMP-9 产量减少相关。重要的是,在不明显改变肺部病毒复制动力学的情况下,TMPRSS2 抑制有助于保持 ECM 完整性,减轻跨上皮电阻 (TEER) 下降,减弱肺泡血管渗漏,并减少受感染小鼠支气管肺泡灌洗液中炎症细胞因子的释放和白细胞浸润。总的来说,这些发现表明 TMPRSS2/PAR-2 轴可能有助于流感相关的肺部病理学,为 宿主 靶向蛋白酶抑制作为减轻严重流感引起的急性肺损伤的潜在策略提供了有用的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒引起的严重急性肺损伤(ALI)以肺泡通透性过高为特征,其中基质金属蛋白酶-9(MMP-9)与细胞外基质(ECM)降解和上皮屏障受损密切相关。
设计与对象
该研究调查了 宿主 蛋白酶 TMPRSS2 及其下游伙伴蛋白酶激活受体 2 (PAR-2) 在 A/WSN/33(H1N1) 感染期间调节 MMP-9 表达的潜在参与。
核心方法
该研究使用 Camostat 对 TMPRSS2 进行药理学抑制,以抑制 PAR-2 激活并评估体外和体内下游激酶磷酸化。
主要结果
我们的研究结果表明,病毒感染伴随着 TMPRSS2 介导的 PAR-2 激活,以及 MEK/ERK 和 PI3K/AKT 信号通路的下游参与以及增强的 NF-κB 活性,与升高的 MMP-9 水平相关。
解释与创新
总的来说,这些发现表明 TMPRSS2/PAR-2 轴可能有助于流感相关的肺部病理学,为 宿主 靶向蛋白酶抑制作为减轻严重流感引起的急性肺损伤的潜在策略提供了有用的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 宿主 靶向蛋白酶抑制策略的开发提供信息,以减轻严重流感引起的急性肺损伤。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告局限性。

Targeting TMPRSS2 protects against influenza-associated lung injury by suppressing PAR-2/MMP-9 axis

Authors: Yin Wu, Ming Yu, Li Feng, Qifeng Peng, Juan Yang, Linsong Hou, Hong Pan, Yi Zhang, Wu Y, Yu M
Original abstract (English)
Severe acute lung injury (ALI) induced by influenza A virus is characterized by alveolar hyperpermeability, in which matrix metalloproteinase-9 (MMP-9) is closely associated with extracellular matrix (ECM) degradation and epithelial barrier compromise. However, the host regulatory mechanism involved in virus-associated MMP-9 upregulation remain incompletely understood. In this study, we investigated the potential involvement of the host protease TMPRSS2 and its downstream partner protease-activated receptor-2 (PAR-2) in modulating MMP-9 expression during A/WSN/33(H1N1) infection. Our findings indicate that viral infection is accompanied by TMPRSS2-mediated PAR-2 activation, alongside downstream engagement of the MEK/ERK and PI3K/AKT signaling pathways and enhanced NF-κB activity, correlating with elevated MMP-9 levels. Pharmacological inhibition of TMPRSS2 with Camostat suppressed PAR-2 activation, attenuated downstream kinase phosphorylation in vitro and in vivo, and was associated with reduced MMP-9 production. Importantly, without apparent alterations in pulmonary viral replication kinetics, TMPRSS2 inhibition helped preserve ECM integrity, mitigated transepithelial electrical resistance (TEER) decline, attenuated alveolar vascular leakage, and reduced inflammatory cytokine release and leukocyte infiltration in bronchoalveolar lavage fluid in infected mice. Collectively, these findings suggest that the TMPRSS2/PAR-2 axis may contribute to influenza-associated lung pathology, providing useful insights into host-targeted protease inhibition as a potential strategy to alleviate severe influenza-induced acute lung injury.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Severe acute lung injury (ALI) induced by influenza A virus is characterized by alveolar hyperpermeability, in which matrix metalloproteinase-9 (MMP-9) is closely associated with extracellular matrix (ECM) degradation and epithelial barrier compromise.
Design and population
The study investigated the potential involvement of the host protease TMPRSS2 and its downstream partner protease-activated receptor-2 (PAR-2) in modulating MMP-9 expression during A/WSN/33(H1N1) infection.
Core methods
The study used pharmacological inhibition of TMPRSS2 with Camostat to suppress PAR-2 activation and assess downstream kinase phosphorylation in vitro and in vivo.
Main results
Our findings indicate that viral infection is accompanied by TMPRSS2-mediated PAR-2 activation, alongside downstream engagement of the MEK/ERK and PI3K/AKT signaling pathways and enhanced NF-κB activity, correlating with elevated MMP-9 levels.
Interpretation and novelty
Collectively, these findings suggest that the TMPRSS2/PAR-2 axis may contribute to influenza-associated lung pathology, providing useful insights into host-targeted protease inhibition as a potential strategy to alleviate severe influenza-induced acute lung injury.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of host-targeted protease inhibition strategies to alleviate severe influenza-induced acute lung injury.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations.

流感类型和亚型动态以及与区域 监测 的时间一致性:儿科住院患者测试记录的 65 个月回顾性分析

Influenza type and subtype dynamics and temporal concordance with regional surveillance: a 65-month retrospective analysis of pediatric inpatient test records
作者： Bing Zhang, Bo Yang, Jia Zhao, Maoji Zhao, Wei Tang, Jiangyu Li, Lijun Du
摘要中文翻译常规分子检测可提供流感类型和亚型结果,但其对于描述住院儿童的长期模式以及将这些模式与区域 监测 进行比较的价值仍不清楚。我们使用 7 天重复数据删除规则,分析了 2021 年 1 月至 2026 年 5 月期间临床医生记录的下呼吸道感染的 17,835 名 0-14 岁儿科和新生儿住院患者的 21,965 份咽拭子检测记录。我们总结了 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和乙型流感的阳性率,并比较了五个完整日历年(2021-2025)的粗略阳性率和年龄标准化阳性率确定的主要类别。我们使用多变量逻辑回归和患者聚类稳健标准误来评估年龄关联。我们将医院的每月类别构成与中国国家流感中心 (CNIC) 的华南地区 监测 汇总数据进行了比较。甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 的阳性率为 2.51%,甲型H3N2流感病毒 的阳性率为 3.34%,B 型流感的阳性率为 2.18%。2021 年至 2025 年间,年度主要类别发生了多次变化,粗略和年龄标准化阳性率在每年内都确定了相同的主要类别。与< 1岁儿童相比,6-14岁儿童的调整优势比分别为1.58、5.94和4.23。类别和年龄组之间的交互作用显着(P<0.001)。在同时定义了两个系列组成的 47 个月份中,三个类别的 Pearson r 值分别为 0.765、0.662 和 0.895。这两个系列在 44 个月中的 35 个月中确定了相同的领先类别,并且两个系列中都有一个独特的领先类别(79.5%,Cohen’s κ = 0.693)。在接受测试的住院患者中,年度主要流感类别发生了多次变化,而且不同类别之间年龄关联的强度也有所不同。医院和 CNIC 华南地区的月度股票在所有三个类别中均呈正相关。常规住院患者检测可以补充区域性流感 监测 的本地、按类型和亚型按年龄分层的检测数据。

问题与背景
常规分子检测可提供流感类型和亚型结果,但其对于描述住院儿童的长期模式以及将这些模式与区域 监测 进行比较的价值仍不清楚。

主要结果
甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 的阳性率为 2.51%,甲型H3N2流感病毒 的阳性率为 3.34%,乙型流感的阳性率为 2.18%。

2021 年至 2025 年间,年度领先类别发生了数次变化,不同类别的年龄关联强度也有所不同。
科研与公卫意义

Influenza type and subtype dynamics and temporal concordance with regional surveillance: a 65-month retrospective analysis of pediatric inpatient test records

Authors: Bing Zhang, Bo Yang, Jia Zhao, Maoji Zhao, Wei Tang, Jiangyu Li, Lijun Du
Original abstract (English)
Routine molecular testing provides influenza type and subtype results, but their value for describing long-term patterns in hospitalized children and comparing these patterns with regional surveillance remains unclear. We analyzed 21,965 throat-swab testing records from 17,835 pediatric and neonatal inpatients aged 0–14 years with clinician-recorded lower respiratory tract infection from January 2021 to May 2026, using a 7-day deduplication rule. We summarized positivity for A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and influenza B and compared the leading categories identified by crude and age-standardized positivity for the five complete calendar years (2021–2025). We assessed age associations using multivariable logistic regression with patient-clustered robust standard errors. We compared the hospital’s monthly category composition with aggregate surveillance data for southern China from the Chinese National Influenza Center (CNIC). Positivity was 2.51% for A(H1N1)pdm09, 3.34% for A(H3N2), and 2.18% for influenza B. Annual leading categories changed several times between 2021 and 2025, and crude and age-standardized positivity identified the same leading category within each year. Compared with children aged < 1 year, adjusted odds ratios for children aged 6–14 years were 1.58, 5.94, and 4.23, respectively. The interaction between category and age group was significant ( P < 0.001). In the 47 concurrent months with composition defined in both series, Pearson r values for the three categories were 0.765, 0.662, and 0.895, respectively. The two series identified the same leading category in 35 of the 44 months with a unique leading category in both series (79.5%, Cohen’s κ = 0.693). Annual leading influenza categories changed several times among tested inpatients, and the strength of the age association differed across categories. The hospital and CNIC southern China monthly shares were positively correlated for all three categories. Routine inpatient testing can supplement regional influenza surveillance with local, age-stratified detection data by type and subtype.

Scientific and public-health significance

The interaction between category and age group was significant (P < 0.001).

地球上丰富的高岭土衍生 抗病毒药物 涂层通过三重协同捕获-失活-阻断机制。

Earth-Abundant Kaolin Derived Antiviral Coating via Triple Synergistic Capture-Inactivate-Block Mechanism.
作者： Jiangpeng Ding, Donghui Sun, Hao Wang, Huaming Yang
摘要中文翻译抗病毒药物 涂层转化受到添加剂聚集和树脂不相容性的限制。我们通过热活化和与铜前体的一锅球磨将天然高岭土升级为高效的 抗病毒药物 添加剂,产生均匀装饰有铜颗粒 (Cu Ps) 的无定形偏高岭土。这种无溶剂方案可抑制 Cu Ps 团聚,并确保有机硅丙烯酸粘合剂中的均匀分散,这对于涂层的完整性至关重要。偏高岭土支架充当高亲和力的病毒捕获平台,而锚定的Cu Ps协同产生超氧自由基(•O2-)并维持局部Cu+/Cu2+释放,从而实现双模式氧化失活。与树脂的物理屏障相结合,这种捕获-氧化-阻断机制在环境条件下可实现 >99.9% 的甲型流感 H3N2 和肠道病毒 EV-71 灭活。该复合材料支持公斤级生产(批量 2.22 公斤),在超过 6000 次洗涤循环后仍保持完整活性,并通过皮肤安全测试。这项工作将低成本粘土矿物转化为结构工程 抗病毒药物 材料,并提供了一种可扩展的路线,克服了表面保护方面持续的可制造性和耐久性限制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 抗病毒药物 涂层开发的局限性,特别是阻碍 抗病毒药物 有效转化的添加剂聚集和树脂不相容性的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用无溶剂方案开发 抗病毒药物 涂料,将天然高岭土升级为高效的 抗病毒药物 添加剂,重点关注其在有机硅丙烯酸粘合剂中的应用。
核心方法
该方法涉及铜前驱体的热活化和一锅球磨,以产生均匀地装饰有铜颗粒的非晶偏高岭土,从而抑制Cu Ps团聚并确保均匀分散。
主要结果
偏高岭土支架表现出高亲和力的病毒捕获能力,而锚定的 Cu Ps 协同产生超氧自由基并持续局部 Cu+/Cu2+ 释放,从而实现双模式氧化失活。
解释与创新
作者将这一发现解释为一种新颖的三重协同捕获-灭活-阻断机制,可在环境条件下有效灭活甲型 H3N2 流感和肠道病毒 EV-71。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了一种可扩展且耐用的 抗病毒药物 涂层解决方案,可以为 季节性流感 病毒表面保护材料的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象水平的报告,不包括详细的实验验证,但由于清楚地证明了该机制及其针对特定目标病毒的有效性,证据是强有力的。

Earth-Abundant Kaolin Derived Antiviral Coating via Triple Synergistic Capture-Inactivate-Block Mechanism.

Authors: Jiangpeng Ding, Donghui Sun, Hao Wang, Huaming Yang
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral coating translation is constrained by additive aggregation and resin incompatibility. We upgrade natural kaolin into an efficient antiviral additive via thermal activation and one-pot ball milling with copper precursors, yielding amorphous metakaolin uniformly decorated with copper particles (Cu Ps). This solvent-free protocol suppresses Cu Ps agglomeration and ensures homogeneous dispersion within silicone-acrylic binders, essential for coating integrity. The metakaolin scaffold serves as a high-affinity viral capture platform, while anchored Cu Ps synergistically generate superoxide radicals (•O2-) and sustain localized Cu+/Cu2+ release, enabling dual-modal oxidative inactivation. Combined with the resin's physical barrier, this capture-oxidize-block mechanism delivers >99.9% inactivation of influenza A H3N2 and enterovirus EV-71 under ambient conditions. The composite supports kilogram-scale production (2.22 kg batch), maintains full activity after >6000 washing cycles, and passes dermal safety tests. This work transforms a low-cost clay mineral into a structurally engineered antiviral material and provides a scalable route that overcomes persistent manufacturability and durability limitations in surface protection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations in antiviral coating development, particularly the challenges of additive aggregation and resin incompatibility that hinder effective antiviral translation.
Design and population
The study involved the development of an antiviral coating using a solvent-free protocol to upgrade natural kaolin into an efficient antiviral additive, with a focus on its application in silicone-acrylic binders.
Core methods
The method involved thermal activation and one-pot ball milling with copper precursors to create amorphous metakaolin uniformly decorated with copper particles, which suppresses Cu Ps agglomeration and ensures homogeneous dispersion.
Main results
The metakaolin scaffold demonstrated high-affinity viral capture, while the anchored Cu Ps synergistically generated superoxide radicals and sustained localized Cu+/Cu2+ release, enabling dual-modal oxidative inactivation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a novel triple synergistic capture-inactivate-block mechanism that effectively inactivates influenza A H3N2 and enterovirus EV-71 under ambient conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a scalable and durable antiviral coating solution that could inform the development of surface protection materials for seasonal influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level reporting and does not include detailed experimental validation, but the evidence is strong due to the clear demonstration of the mechanism and its effectiveness against specified target viruses.

接受生物治疗的严重哮喘成人中的毒株特异性流感 疫苗 免疫原性:一项真实世界基线调整的 HAI 研究

Strain-specific influenza vaccine immunogenicity in adults with severe asthma receiving biologic therapy: a real-world baseline-adjusted HAI study
作者： Aristitsa Mikhailovna Kostinova, М. П. Костинов, Lilia Gladkova, Irina Leonidovna Soloveva, Al’bina Khasanova, Хамидулина Анна Анатольевна, Anton Kostinov, Anna Shmitko, Natalia Andreeva, В. Б. Полищук
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 关于流感 疫苗 治疗严重哮喘的生物制剂 疫苗 免疫原性的证据仍然有限,特别是在常规临床实践中治疗的成人中。
设计与对象
在这项前瞻性单中心观察性研究中,我们分析了 2023-2024 年期间接受 Grippol 四价治疗的 147 名成人哮喘患者在 疫苗接种 治疗前和治疗后的血凝抑制 (HAI) 滴度:126 名严重哮喘患者正在接受持续的生物治疗,21 名哮喘对照患者未接受生物治疗。
核心方法
在疫苗接种之前和疫苗接种之后4-6周采集血液。对于每个 疫苗 菌株,使用研究组的协方差分析对 log2(疫苗接种 HAI 后)进行建模,并将相应的 log2(疫苗接种 HAI 前)滴度作为预测因子。
主要结果
结果 调整后的 GMR 分别为:A/H1N1/维多利亚为 0.61 (95% CI 0.33–1.13;P = 0.113),A/H3N2/达尔文为 1.08 (0.64–1.81;P = 0.779),A/H3N2/达尔文为 0.46 (0.23–0.94;P = 0.032)。B/奥地利,B/普吉岛为 0.59 (0.34–1.05;P = 0.073)。
解释与创新
将组系数反向转换为调整后的几何平均比(GMR;生物治疗/对照)。这些观察数据将随机证据从个体生物制剂扩展到现实世界的成人严重哮喘 队列研究。
科研与公卫意义
Baseline-adjusted estimates differed numerically across strains, but between-strain heterogeneity was not formally tested. B/Austria yielded a nominally lower adjusted titre (P = 0.032; Holm-adjusted P = 0.128), but not after additional adjustment for age and sex (GMR 0.54, 95% CI 0.28–1.05; P = 0.068); confidence intervals for the other three strains included unity; all findings are exploratory.
局限与证据强度
所有发现都是探索性的,其他三种菌株的置信区间包括统一性。该研究是观察性的,并未正式测试菌株间的异质性。

Strain-specific influenza vaccine immunogenicity in adults with severe asthma receiving biologic therapy: a real-world baseline-adjusted HAI study

Authors: Aristitsa Mikhailovna Kostinova, М. П. Костинов, Lilia Gladkova, Irina Leonidovna Soloveva, Al’bina Khasanova, Хамидулина Анна Анатольевна, Anton Kostinov, Anna Shmitko, Natalia Andreeva, В. Б. Полищук
Original abstract (English)
Background Evidence on influenza vaccine immunogenicity during biologic treatment for severe asthma remains limited, particularly in adults treated in routine clinical practice. Methods In this prospective single-centre observational study, we analysed pre- and post-vaccination haemagglutination-inhibition (HAI) titres in 147 adults with asthma who received Grippol Quadrivalent during the 2023–2024 season: 126 patients with severe asthma receiving ongoing biologic therapy and 21 asthma controls not receiving biologic therapy. Blood was obtained before vaccination and 4–6 weeks after vaccination. For each vaccine strain, log2(post-vaccination HAI) was modelled using analysis of covariance with study group and the corresponding log2(pre-vaccination HAI) titre as predictors. The group coefficient was back-transformed to an adjusted geometric mean ratio (GMR; biologic therapy/control). Results Adjusted GMRs were 0.61 (95% CI 0.33–1.13; P = 0.113) for A/H1N1/Victoria, 1.08 (0.64–1.81; P = 0.779) for A/H3N2/Darwin, 0.46 (0.23–0.94; P = 0.032) for B/Austria, and 0.59 (0.34–1.05; P = 0.073) for B/Phuket. Conclusion Baseline-adjusted estimates differed numerically across strains, but between-strain heterogeneity was not formally tested.B/Austria yielded a nominally lower adjusted titre (P = 0.032; Holm-adjusted P = 0.128), but not after additional adjustment for age and sex (GMR 0.54, 95% CI 0.28–1.05; P = 0.068); confidence intervals for the other three strains included unity; all findings are exploratory. These observational data extend randomized evidence from individual biologics to an adult real-world severe-asthma cohort.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Evidence on influenza vaccine immunogenicity during biologic treatment for severe asthma remains limited, particularly in adults treated in routine clinical practice.
Design and population
In this prospective single-centre observational study, we analysed pre- and post-vaccination haemagglutination-inhibition (HAI) titres in 147 adults with asthma who received Grippol Quadrivalent during the 2023–2024 season: 126 patients with severe asthma receiving ongoing biologic therapy and 21 asthma controls not receiving biologic therapy.

Main results
Results Adjusted GMRs were 0.61 (95% CI 0.33–1.13; P = 0.113) for A/H1N1/Victoria, 1.08 (0.64–1.81; P = 0.779) for A/H3N2/Darwin, 0.46 (0.23–0.94; P = 0.032) for B/Austria, and 0.59 (0.34–1.05; P = 0.073) for B/Phuket.
Interpretation and novelty
The group coefficient was back-transformed to an adjusted geometric mean ratio (GMR; biologic therapy/control). These observational data extend randomized evidence from individual biologics to an adult real-world severe-asthma cohort.

Baseline-adjusted estimates differed numerically across strains, but between-strain heterogeneity was not formally tested. B/Austria yielded a nominally lower adjusted titre (P = 0.032; Holm-adjusted P = 0.128), but not after additional adjustment for age and sex (GMR 0.54, 95% CI 0.28–1.05; P = 0.068); confidence intervals for the other three strains included unity; all findings are exploratory.
Limitations and evidence strength
All findings are exploratory, and confidence intervals for the other three strains included unity. The study is observational and does not formally test between-strain heterogeneity.

莆田市19382名住院儿童11种呼吸道病原体流行病学特征

Epidemiological characteristics of 11 respiratory pathogens among 19,382 hospitalized children in Putian city
作者： Yusi Xin, 俞柳敏, Qi Zeng, 林星宇, Youdan Ruan, 林华, Zhanfei Chen
摘要中文翻译目的分析莆田市急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)住院儿童常见呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征,为区域小儿呼吸道感染的预防提供科学依据。方法 回顾性分析2023年1月1日至2025年12月30日莆田学院附属医院住院的19 382例儿童的临床资料,采用多重聚合酶链式反应(PCR)结合毛细管电泳片段分析法检测11种常见呼吸道病原菌。结果共纳入住院儿童19382例,呼吸道病原总体阳性率为69.40%。学龄前儿童的阳性率最高,青少年最低。学龄儿童中肺炎支原体(Mp)检出率最高(38.57%),婴幼儿中人呼吸道合胞病毒(HRSV)检出​​率最高(19.53%)。男性病原体阳性率为70.24%,女性病原体阳性率为68.20%,性别间差异有统计学意义。19382例ARTI儿童中,单一病原检测为主,占58.89%;双病原检测占9.93%,多病原检测占0.57%。呼吸道病原体阳性率呈现季节性波动,峰值出现在2023年冬季(73.98%)、2024年春季(77.85%)和2025年秋季(74.17%)。此外,甲型流感病毒(InfA)、乙型流感病毒(InfB)的阳性率随温度升高而降低,而HRSV、人类冠状病毒(HCOV)的阳性率随温度升高而升高。A total of 15,601 pathogen detection events were recorded;三种最常见的病原体是人鼻病毒(HRV;24.66%)、Mp(23.68%)和HRSV(14.52%)。结论 莆田市ARTIs住院儿童呼吸道病原体总体阳性率较高(69.40%),且具有明显的年龄和季节特征。Mp predominated in school-aged children,whereas HRSV predominated in infants and toddlers. Pathogen detection peaked in autumn–winter 2023,spring 2024,and autumn 2025. HRV,Mp,and HRSV were the three dominant pathogens.双重和多重检测组的中位住院时间为 6 天,而病原体阴性和单一检测组的中位住院时间为 5 天。这些研究结果为优化该地区小儿呼吸道感染临床诊断和治疗提供了科学依据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析莆田市急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)住院儿童常见呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征,为区域小儿呼吸道感染的预防提供科学依据。
设计与对象
对2023年1月1日至2025年12月30日莆田学院附属医院住院的19382名儿童的临床资料进行回顾性分析。
核心方法
采用多重聚合酶链反应(PCR)结合毛细管电泳片段分析检测11种常见呼吸道病原体。
主要结果
共纳入19382名住院儿童,呼吸道病原体总体阳性率为69.40%。学龄前儿童的阳性率最高,青少年最低。学龄儿童中肺炎支原体(Mp)检出率最高(38.57%),婴幼儿中人呼吸道合胞病毒(HRSV)检出​​率最高(19.53%)。呼吸道病原体阳性率呈现季节性波动,峰值出现在2023年冬季(73.98%)、2024年春季(77.85%)和2025年秋季(74.17%)。
解释与创新
该研究强调了莆田市 ARTI 住院儿童呼吸道病原体与年龄和季节的明显特征,为这些病原体的分布模式提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些研究结果为优化该地区小儿呼吸道感染临床诊断和治疗提供了科学依据。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,这可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。由于该研究的观察性质,证据是中等的。

Epidemiological characteristics of 11 respiratory pathogens among 19,382 hospitalized children in Putian city

Authors: Yusi Xin, 俞柳敏, Qi Zeng, 林星宇, Youdan Ruan, 林华, Zhanfei Chen
Original abstract (English)
Objective To analyze the epidemiological characteristics of common respiratory pathogens among children hospitalized with acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs) in Putian city with the aim of providing a scientific basis for regional pediatric respiratory infection prevention. Methods A retrospective analysis was conducted on clinical data from 19,382 children hospitalized at the Affiliated Hospital of Putian University between January 1, 2023, and December 30, 2025. Multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) combined with capillary electrophoresis fragment analysis was employed to detect 11 common respiratory pathogens. Results A total of 19,382 hospitalized children were enrolled, with an overall respiratory pathogen positivity rate of 69.40%. The positivity rate was highest among preschool-aged children and lowest among adolescents. Mycoplasma pneumoniae (Mp) exhibited the highest detection rate in school-aged children (38.57%), while human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) demonstrated the highest detection rate in infants and toddlers (19.53%). The pathogen positivity rate was 70.24% in males and 68.20% in females, with a statistically significant difference between sexes. Among the 19,382 children with ARTIs, single-pathogen detection predominated, accounting for 58.89%; dual-pathogen detection accounted for 9.93%, and multiple-pathogen detection accounted for 0.57%. Respiratory pathogen positivity rates showed seasonal fluctuations, peaking in winter 2023 (73.98%), spring 2024 (77.85%), and autumn 2025 (74.17%). In addition, the positivity rates of influenza A virus (InfA), influenza B virus (InfB) decreased with increasing temperature, whereas those of HRSV, human coronavirus (HCOV) increased with increasing temperature. A total of 15,601 pathogen detection events were recorded; the three most frequently detected pathogens were human rhinovirus (HRV; 24.66%), Mp (23.68%), and HRSV (14.52%). Conclusion The overall positivity rate of respiratory pathogens among children hospitalized with ARTIs in Putian was high (69.40%), with distinct age-related and seasonal characteristics. Mp predominated in school-aged children, whereas HRSV predominated in infants and toddlers. Pathogen detection peaked in autumn–winter 2023, spring 2024, and autumn 2025. HRV, Mp, and HRSV were the three dominant pathogens. The dual- and multiple-detection groups had median hospital stays of 6 days, compared with 5 days in the pathogen-negative and single-detection groups. These findings provide a scientific basis for optimizing clinical diagnosis and treatment of pediatric respiratory infections in this region.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze the epidemiological characteristics of common respiratory pathogens among children hospitalized with acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs) in Putian city to provide a scientific basis for regional pediatric respiratory infection prevention.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on clinical data from 19,382 children hospitalized at the Affiliated Hospital of Putian University between January 1, 2023, and December 30, 2025.
Core methods
Multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) combined with capillary electrophoresis fragment analysis was employed to detect 11 common respiratory pathogens.
Main results
A total of 19,382 hospitalized children were enrolled, with an overall respiratory pathogen positivity rate of 69.40%. The positivity rate was highest among preschool-aged children and lowest among adolescents. Mycoplasma pneumoniae (Mp) exhibited the highest detection rate in school-aged children (38.57%), while human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) demonstrated the highest detection rate in infants and toddlers (19.53%). Respiratory pathogen positivity rates showed seasonal fluctuations, peaking in winter 2023 (73.98%), spring 2024 (77.85%), and autumn 2025 (74.17%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the distinct age-related and seasonal characteristics of respiratory pathogens among children hospitalized with ARTIs in Putian, providing novel insights into the distribution patterns of these pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide a scientific basis for optimizing clinical diagnosis and treatment of pediatric respiratory infections in this region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data, which may introduce bias and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

疫苗 在重症肌无力和 CIDP 新型免疫疗法下对流感病毒和 SARS-CoV-2 的反应:一项前瞻性观察研究

Vaccine responses to influenza viruses and SARS-CoV-2 under novel immunotherapies in myasthenia gravis and CIDP: a prospective observational study
作者： Seyed Siyawasch Justus Lattau, Fee Aliah Heiler, Stefanie Glaubitz, Stefanie Meyer, Ralf Dürrwald, Dirk Fitzner, Melanie Sylvia Eisele, Jana Zschüntzsch
摘要中文翻译摘要 重症肌无力(MG)和慢性炎症性脱髓鞘性多发性神经病(CIDP)患者需要长期免疫治疗,因此有效的疫苗接种对于感染预防至关重要。由于这些免疫疗法越来越多地涉及 FcRn 拮抗剂和补体抑制剂等新型药物,因此需要评估它们对 疫苗 反应的影响。因此,我们前瞻性地招募了 23 名接受当代免疫疗法的 MG 或 CIDP 患者,这些患者接种了流感病毒 (n = 21;北半球 2025/26) 或 SARS-CoV-2 (n = 12) 疫苗。对 疫苗接种 之前和之后至少两周收集的血清进行了针对 SARS-CoV-2 刺突 S1 亚基和三种流感病毒株的抗体检测。12 名患者中有 9 名观察到对 SARS-CoV-2 疫苗接种 的反应(抗 S1 IgG 增加≥ 2 倍)(75%;中位倍数变化 2.6),所有三种毒株的流感血清保护率(血凝抑制 [HAI] 效价≥ 1:40)均上升,达到 86% (A/H1N1)、76% (B/维多利亚)和 67%(A/H3N2)。通过协方差分析 (ANCOVA)、调整基线滴度和 诊断 来评估个体药物对 疫苗接种 后滴度的影响。在这些探索性模型中,ravulizumab 的估计值明显较高,而 efgartigimod 和麦考酚酯的估计值则较低;经过多次测试校正后,没有一个保持统计学显着性。同时接受 efgartigimod 和麦考酚酯治疗的患者在 疫苗接种 治疗后的 A/H1N1 HAI 滴度绝对值显着降低(中位数 1:30 vs. 1:160;p = 0.0085)和 A/H3N2(中位数 < 1:10 vs.)。这些结果与较低的基线和循环抗体水平一致,并且在这项小型探索性研究中没有显示出可检测到的倍数变化差异。队列研究 未能血清转化为任何毒株的患者更频繁地接受 FcRn 拮抗剂,但大多数患者在 FcRn 拮抗作用下保留了针对至少一种毒株的血清保护,尽管总 IgG 同时下降,但仍有几名患者发生了血清转化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估接受免疫治疗的重症肌无力(MG)和慢性炎症性脱髓鞘性多发性神经病(CIDP)患者对季节性流感病毒和SARS-CoV-2的疫苗反应,因为这些患者需要长期治疗,而有效的疫苗接种对于预防感染至关重要。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察性研究招募了 23 名接受当代免疫疗法的 MG 或 CIDP 患者,他们接种了流感病毒疫苗(n = 21;北半球 2025/26)或 SARS-CoV-2(n = 12)。
核心方法
使用协方差分析 (ANCOVA)、调整基线滴度和 诊断 来评估个体药物对 疫苗接种 后抗体滴度的影响。
主要结果
12 名患者中有 9 名观察到对 SARS-CoV-2 疫苗接种 的反应(抗 S1 IgG 增加≥2 倍)(75%;中位倍数变化 2.6),所有三种病毒株的流感血清保护率(HAI 滴度≥1:40)均增加,达到 86%(A/H1N1)、76%(B/维多利亚)和 67% (A/H3N2)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管接受了当代免疫治疗,大多数患者对 疫苗 都产生了可测量的体液反应,这强调了了解 疫苗 在免疫功能低下人群中疗效的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对于接受免疫治疗的自身免疫性疾病患者,特别是 季节性流感 和 SARS-CoV-2,需要制定量身定制的 疫苗接种 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究是探索性的,队列研究 较小,经过多次检验校正后,所有估计值均未保持统计显着性,表明证据强度有限。

Vaccine responses to influenza viruses and SARS-CoV-2 under novel immunotherapies in myasthenia gravis and CIDP: a prospective observational study

Authors: Seyed Siyawasch Justus Lattau, Fee Aliah Heiler, Stefanie Glaubitz, Stefanie Meyer, Ralf Dürrwald, Dirk Fitzner, Melanie Sylvia Eisele, Jana Zschüntzsch
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Patients with myasthenia gravis (MG) and chronic inflammatory demyelinating polyneuropathy (CIDP) require long-term immunotherapy, making effective vaccination essential for infection prophylaxis. As these immunotherapies increasingly involve novel agents such as FcRn antagonists and complement inhibitors, their influence on the vaccine response needs to be assessed. Therefore, we prospectively enrolled 23 patients with MG or CIDP receiving contemporary immunotherapies who were vaccinated against influenza viruses ( n = 21; Northern Hemisphere 2025/26) or SARS-CoV-2 ( n = 12). Sera collected before and at least two weeks after vaccination were assayed for antibodies against the SARS-CoV-2 spike S1 subunit and three influenza strains. A response to SARS-CoV-2 vaccination (≥ 2-fold increase in anti-S1 IgG) was observed in 9 of 12 patients (75%; median fold-change 2.6), and influenza seroprotection rates (haemagglutination inhibition [HAI] titer ≥ 1:40) rose for all three strains reaching 86% (A/H1N1), 76% (B/Victoria), and 67% (A/H3N2). The effect of individual medications on post-vaccination titers was assessed by analysis of covariance (ANCOVA), adjusting for baseline titer and diagnosis. In these exploratory models, estimates were directionally higher under ravulizumab and lower under efgartigimod and mycophenolate; none remained statistically significant after correction for multiple testing. Patients receiving efgartigimod and mycophenolate concurrently had significantly lower absolute post-vaccination HAI titers for A/H1N1 (median 1:30 vs. 1:160; p = 0.0085) and A/H3N2 (median < 1:10 vs. These findings are consistent with lower baseline and circulating antibody levels and do not show a detectable difference in fold-change in this small exploratory cohort. Patients who failed to seroconvert to any strain were more frequently receiving FcRn antagonists, yet most retained seroprotection against at least one strain. Under FcRn antagonism, influenza titers did not parallel total serum IgG, with several patients seroconverting despite a concurrent decline in total IgG. In conclusion, most patients mounted a measurable humoral response to both vaccines despite contemporary immunotherapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess vaccine responses to seasonal influenza viruses and SARS-CoV-2 in patients with myasthenia gravis (MG) and chronic inflammatory demyelinating polyneuropathy (CIDP) undergoing immunotherapy, as these patients require long-term treatment and effective vaccination is essential for infection prevention.
Design and population
This prospective observational study enrolled 23 patients with MG or CIDP receiving contemporary immunotherapies, who were vaccinated against influenza viruses (n = 21; Northern Hemisphere 2025/26) or SARS-CoV-2 (n = 12).
Core methods
The effect of individual medications on post-vaccination antibody titers was evaluated using analysis of covariance (ANCOVA), adjusting for baseline titer and diagnosis.
Main results
A response to SARS-CoV-2 vaccination (≥ 2-fold increase in anti-S1 IgG) was observed in 9 of 12 patients (75%; median fold-change 2.6), and influenza seroprotection rates (HAI titer ≥ 1:40) increased for all three strains, reaching 86% (A/H1N1), 76% (B/Victoria), and 67% (A/H3N2).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that most patients mounted a measurable humoral response to both vaccines despite receiving contemporary immunotherapy, highlighting the importance of understanding vaccine efficacy in immunocompromised populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for tailored vaccination strategies in patients with autoimmune diseases undergoing immunotherapy, particularly for seasonal influenza and SARS-CoV-2.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is exploratory with a small cohort, and none of the estimates remained statistically significant after multiple testing correction, indicating limited evidence strength.
